1b1(mdo sde): @##//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya bod+hi 
1b2(mdo sde): satwa go tsa ro prA ya bi Sha ya bi ki r+thi 
1b3(mdo sde): ta ni rde sha nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra/bod skad du 
2a1(mdo sde): @##//'phags pa bang chung sems dpa'i spyod 
2a2(mdo sde): yul gyi thang sa kyi yul la rnam par 'phrul pa 
2a3(mdo sde): bstan pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo//
2a4(mdo sde): sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad 
2b1(mdo sde): la phag 'tshal le//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/
2b2(mdo sde): bcom ldan 'das yul 'phags rgyal na/rgyal po gtum po rab 
2b3(mdo sde): snang gi bskyed mes tshal shings la dang /ta la dang /ta 
2b4(mdo sde): me la dang /de dga'i shing dang /n+ya gro d+ha dang /rA ga sha dang /
2b5(mdo sde): u dum wa ra dang /par shi ka dang /d+ha nu ska ri dang /a ti mug ta 
3a1(mdo sde): @##//ka dang /tsam pa ka dang /mya ngan mtshang dang /skya snar gyis 
3a2(mdo sde): legs par brgyan pa/'bab chu'i dngo dang /mtsho dang /mtsho bran dang /mtshe'u 
3a3(mdo sde): dang /rdzing dang /lu ma mang pos mdzes par byas pa/me tog ud pa 
3a4(mdo sde): la dang /pad ma dang /ku mu da dang /pad ma dkar pos mngon par gang ba/
3a5(mdo sde): ngang pa dang /khrung khrung dang /rma bya dang /khu byug dang /ku na la dang /
3b1(mdo sde): 'dab skyob dang /ne tso dang /ri skegs dang /shang shang te'u dag sgra mngon par sgrog pa dang 
3b2(mdo sde): ldan pa/bung ba mang pos gang ba/sa gzhi ne'u gsing sngo ba dang ldan pa'i nags tshal chen po 
3b3(mdo sde): mtshog na dge slong khri nyis stong gi dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /thabs gcig tu bzhugs 
3b4(mdo sde): te/'di lta ste/tshe dang ldan pa shI ra'i bu dang /mo'u dag la gyi bu chen po dang /
3b5(mdo sde): 'od srung chen po dang /ma 'gags pa dang /rab 'byor dang /ka t+ya'i bu chen po dang /ka ba 
3b6(mdo sde): pi na chen po dang /nag pa dang /dga' bo dang /lteng rgyas 'od srung dang /chu klung 'od srung 
4a1(mdo sde): @##//dang /ga ya 'od srung dang /byams ma'i bu gang po dang /ba lang bdag dang /
4a2(mdo sde): ba ku la dang /lam bstan dang /lam phran bstan dang /gyad bu nor dang /seng ldeng 
4a3(mdo sde): nags pa dang /skula byed dang /gsus po che dang /sgra gcan zin dang /tshe dang ldan pa 
4a4(mdo sde): kun dga' bo dang /de dag la sogs pa dge slong khri nyisa stong dang /thabs gcig 
4a5(mdo sde): ste;thams cad kyang /chos kyi dbyings tshul gcig pa'i spyod yul la gnas pa ngo bo nyid kyi chos kyi 
4a6(mdo sde): tshul la zhugs pa/namakha' gnas med pa'i spyod yul pa/mi rten par gnas pa nyon mongs pa dang /
4a7(mdo sde): sgrib pa dang /chos pa dang /kun nas bkris pa thams cad dang bral ba/de bzhin gshegs pa 
4b1(mdo sde): chos kyi dbyings snang ba'i tshul la zhugs pa/chos thams cad tshul gcig pa la yang dag par gzhol ba/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la 
4b2(mdo sde): mngon par phyogs pa/lam gyi rgyud ma bcad pa/thams cad mkhyen pa nyidal mos pa mi zlogs pa'i yid dang ldan pa/shes rab 
4b3(mdo sde): dang /ye shes rnam par mthong ba'i dam pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa/thabs kyi yul dang /spyod yul la nges par 'byung ba sha
4b4(mdo sde): stag go/dge slong ma skye dgu'i bdag mo chen mo dang /grags 'dzin la sogs pa dge slong ma brgyad 'bum dang yang 
4b5(mdo sde): thabs gcig ste/chos dkar po shin tu yongs su bsdus pa/thams cad mkhyen pa'i nyid kyi lam rab tu rtogs 
4b6(mdo sde): pa/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes dang nye bar gyur pa/dngos po med pa'i chos kyi tshul shin tu rtogs pa/mtshan 
4b7(mdo sde): nyid med pa'i chos kyi tshul la rtog pa/chos thams cad yang dag pa'i mthas mtha' med par shin tu mos pa/sgrib pa 
4b8(mdo sde): med pa'i rnam par thar pa thob pa/gdul ba'i 'gro ba ji lta ba de bzhin du gzugs kun tu ston pa la mkhas pa sha stag go/
5a1(mdo sde): @##//byang chub sems dpa' sa ya phrag bdun dang /nyi 'bum gyi byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun chen po dang 
5a2(mdo sde): yang thabs gcig ste /'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shugs chen dang /shugs chen 'dzin dang /
5a3(mdo sde): rnam par 'phrul ba chen po dang /rnam par 'phrul ba chen po'i rgyal po dang /brtson 'grus chen pos rnam par gnon pa dang /
5a4(mdo sde): rtsal chen dpa' bo dang /cher bsgyings dang /cher bsgyings stobs ldan dang /tshogs chen dbang po dang /glang 
5a5(mdo sde): po chen po spos kyi glang po dang /zla ba chen po dang /zla ba bzang po dang /yon tan zla ba dang /rin chen zla ba dang /
5a6(mdo sde): kun tu snang ba'i zla ba dang /chos dri ma med pa'i zla ba dang /rnam par snang byed kyi zla ba dang /rnam par grags pa'i zla ba dang /
5a7(mdo sde): 'od zer rab tu 'gyed pa'i zla ba dang /rgyas pa'i zla ba dang /tshangs pa'i dbyangs dang /tshangs pa'i dbang po ltar sgrog pa'i 
5b1(mdo sde): dbyangs dang /sa la nges par sgrog pa'i dbyangs dang /chos kyi dbyings nges par sgrog pa'i dbyang dang /bdud kyi dkyil
5b2(mdo sde): 'khor thams cad sma 'bebs pa'i dbyangs dang /chosakyi sgra nges par sgrog pa'i dbyangad dang /thams cad go bar byed pa'i
5b3(mdo sde): dbyangs dang /rnam par mi rtog pas/rnam par ma rtags pa'i dbyangs dang /sa steng nges par sgrog pa'i dbyangang dang /
5b4(mdo sde): dbyangs kyi yan lag thams cad zil gyis gnon pa'i dbyangs dang /kun nas snying po dang /yon tan snying po dang /
5b5(mdo sde): chos dri ma med pa'i snying po dang /rnam par snang byed kyi snying po dang /rin chen snying po dang /zla ba'i snying po dang /nyi ma'i snying po dang /snang
5b6(mdo sde): gnyi snying po dang /pad+ma'i snying po dang /pad+ma dpal gyi snying po dang /bsod nams dpal gyi snying po dang /ye shes dpal gyi snying po
5b7(mdo sde): dang /blo gros chen po dang /khyad par blo gros dang /rnam grangs blo gros dang /rlabs chen blo gros dang /bla ma'i blo gros dang /rin chen
6a1(mdo sde): @#//blo gros dang /'phel ba'i blo gros dang /mtha' yas blo gros dang /rgya chen blo gros dang /sangs rgyas blo gros dang /blo gros mi zad pa dang /blo gros rgya mtsho dang /
6a2(mdo sde): lhun po'i sgron ma dang /sgron ma chen po dang /chos ta la la'i sgron ma dang /phyogs thams cad rgyas par 'gengs pa'i sgron ma dang /kun tu sgron ma dang /mun pa thams 
6a3(mdo sde): cad sel ba'i sgron ma dang /'gro ba thams cad snang bar byed pa'i sgron ma dang /shin tu snang ba'i sgron ma dang /zla ba'i sgron ma dang /nyi ma'i sgron ma dang /ngan song 
6a4(mdo sde): sil dang /bdud pa ma byed dang /bdud chen pham byed dang /mngon par 'byung bka' dang /thub dka' dang /gting dpag dka' dang /blo rtog bka' dang /
6a5(mdo sde): ngan 'gro skems dang /mthu chen thob dang /spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug dang /byams pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'jam dpal 
6a6(mdo sde): gzhon nur gyur pa dang /de dag la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' sa ya phrag bdun dang 'bum phrag gnyis dang thabs gcig ste/thams cad kyang skye ba gcig gis thogs pa /
6a7(mdo sde): gzungs thob pa/ting nge 'dzin thob pa/spobs pa mtha' yas pa thob pa/thogs pa med pa'i mi 'jigs pa thob pa/rdzu 'phrul la dbang ba/dam pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa 
6a8(mdo sde): sangs rgyas kyi zhing mtha' yas par 'gro ba'i mngon par shes pas rnam par rtse ba/lus dang sems rnam par grol ba/chags pa med cing sgrib pa med pa'i ye shes mthong ba 
6b1(mdo sde): 'apags shing/ri nags po gzhan zil gyis mnan nas/lam me lhan ne lhang nge ba de bzhin du/bcom ldan 'das gdan seng ge'i khri la bzhugs pa yang/lha dang mir bcas pa'i 'jig rten zil
6b2(mdo sde): gyis mnan cing/lam me lhan ne lhang pe'o//'di lta ste dper na/zla ba yar gyi ngo'i zla ba nya la bab ste/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor rgyas pa ni/rgyu skar dang/skar ma'i tshogs thams cad zil
6b3(mdo sde): gyis mnan te/mog mog por byas nas/lha ma me lhan ne lhang nge'o//de bzhin du bcom ldan 'das gdan seng+ge'i khri la bzhugs pa yang /lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten zil 
6b4(mdo sde): gyis mnan te/mog mog par mdzad nas/lam me lhan ne ltar nge'o//'di lta ste dper na /dag cing dri ma med pa'i nam mkha' sprin med pa la nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor 'od zer gyi dra ba rab 
6b5(mdo sde): tu 'phro bar 'dug pa ni/srin bu me khyer thams cad zil gyis mnan te/mog mog bor byas nas/shin tu lham me lhan ne lhang nge'o//de bzhin du bcom 
6b6(mdo sde): ldan 'das gdan seng ge'i khri la bzhugs pa yang/brgya byin dang/tshangs pa dang /'jig rten skyong ba rnams kyi 'od zil gyis mnan te/mgo mgo bor mdzad nas/lam me lhag ne 
6b7(mdo sde): lhang nge'o//'di lta ste dperan/mtshan me ri chen po'i spo la me chen po 'bar ba ni/lam me lhan ne lhang nge'o/de bzhin du bcom ldan 'angs gdan seng ge'i khri la zhugs pa 
6b8(mdo sde): yang/lam me lhan ne lhang ngo'o//'di lta ste dper na/nor bu rin po che bai DUr+Ya yan lag brgyad dang ldan zhing dri ma med pa/'od zer rab tu 'phro ba ni lam me lhan ne lhang nge'o //de 
8a1(mdo sde): @#//bzhin du bcom ldan 'das gdan seng ge'i khri la bzhugs shing /phyogs bcu thams cad snang bar mdzad pa yang lam me lhan ne lhang nge'o//'di lta ste dper na/
8a2(mdo sde): 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po ni gling bzhi'i sems can thams cad zil gyis mnan cing lam me lhan ne lhang nge'o//de bzhin du bcom ldan 'das gdan seng ge'i khri la bzhug pa 
8a3(mdo sde): yang lam me lhan ne lhangange'o//'di lta ste dper na/lha'i dbang po brgya byin thogs pa'i nor bu rin po che thogs shing /lha'i mdun sa chos bzangs na gnas pa ni/lha thams 
8a4(mdo sde): cad zil gyis mnan cing/lam me lhan ne lhang nge'o //de bzhin du bcom ldan 'das gdan seng ge'i khri la bzhugsa pa yang lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten zil gyis 
8a5(mdo sde): gnan cing /lam me lhan ne lha nge'o//de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas /de lta bu'i 'khor chen po 'dus par yang rig cing/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku bsnyen par dka' 
8a6(mdo sde): ba de yang mthong nas /'di snyam du sems te/'di ltar bcom ldan 'das gdan seng ge'i khri la bzhugs pa yang /shin tu lam me lhane lhang nge'o//'khor chen po 'di yang 
8a7(mdo sde): 'dus par gyur na/'di go ci'i snga ltas zhig tu 'gyur//bdag gis de'i don de bzhin gshegs pa la zhu bar bya'o snyam nas /de'i tshe 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa stan las 
8a8(mdo sde): langs nas/bla gos prag pa gcig tu bzar te/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsug nas/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud de/bcom 
8b1(mdo sde): ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis mngon par bstod do//stobs bcu mnga' ba lhar bcas 'jig rten ni/zil gyis mnan nas khyod ni lhan ner bzhugs/srid pa gsum na 
8b2(mdo sde): khyod 'dra ma mchis na/lhag pa'i sems can mchis par ga la 'gyur/dper na lha gnas gyur pa lhun po ni//ri bo kun gyi nang nas mngon 'phags te//ri bo gzhan dag zil gyis 
8b3(mdo sde): gnan nas su//phyogs rnams kun nas lhan ner rnam par mdzes//de bzhin thub pa yon tan mnga' ba po//ye shes ri bo dbang po'i 'jig rten las//mngon par 'phags 
8b4(mdo sde): shing 'khor kun zil gnan te//rtag tu lhan ner bzhugs shing rnam par mdzes//dper na mkha' la gnas pa'i dgung zla ni//mar gyi ngo la mdzes pa ma lags kyi//dpal 
8b5(mdo sde): ldan dgung zla nya ba rgyas pa ni//skar ma'i tshogs rnams thams cad zil gyis gnon//de bzhin stobs bcu mnga' ba thub pa khyod/rang gi slob ma skar tshogs 
8b6(mdo sde): zham ring mnga'//'jig rten thams cad zil gyis gnon mdzad cing//ye shes 'od rnams kyis kyang sim bar mdzad//drang song dper na gdugs kyi dkyil 'khor ni//
8b7(mdo sde): 'jig rten thams cad zil mnan rab tu lhan//mi yi nyi ma khyod kyang de bzhin te/blo gros 'od kyis 'jig rten kun zil gnon//dper na ri mchog mchis pa'i zhugs kyi 
8b8(mdo sde): tshogs//gzi brjid kyis ni phyogs su rnam par mdzes//de bzhin ye shes ri bzhugs thub pa yang //phyogs bcu dag tu rnam par mdzes pa lags//dper na ri dgas 
9a1(mdo sde): @#//rgyal pos ri dgas phran//zil gyis mnan cing der gnas mngon bar mdzes//mu stegs can gzhan kun gyi nang gnas shing//thub pa khyod kyang de bzhin rnam 
9a2(mdo sde): par mdzes//dper na 'di na mi bdag 'khor los sgyur //'gro ba zil gyis mnan cing rnam par mdzes//mgon po chos mchog 'khor los sgyur ba yang//'gro ba'i nang na de 
9a3(mdo sde): bzhin rnam par mdzes//dper na sum bcu gsum dbang brgya byin ni//lha rnams zil gyis mnan nas mngon par mdzes//mtshungs zla ma mchis de bzhin tshogs pa rnams//
9a4(mdo sde): thems cad zil gyis mnan nas rnam par mdzes//de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas//bcom ldan 'das la migs su bcad pa de dag gis mngon par 
9a5(mdo sde): bstod nas thal mo sbyar te/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das gang thos pas/rgya chen po la mos pa'i sems can rnams 
9a6(mdo sde): bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed par 'gyur ba dang/dman pa la mos pa'i sems can rnas kyang rgya chen po la 'jug pa la gnas par 'gyur ba dang/
9a7(mdo sde): byang chub tu sems bskyed pa'i sems can rnams kyang byang chub la dmigs pa rnam par 'phel bar 'gyur ba dang//dman pa la sbyor ba'i sems can rnams kyang brtsams pa 
9a8(mdo sde): chen po brtsom par 'gyur ba dang /lam las nyams pa'i sems can rnams kyangabyang chub kyi lam du 'jug par 'gyur ba dang/byang chub kyi lam du zhugs pa'i sems 
9b1(mdo sde): can rnams kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi rnam par bsgrub pa'i dgongs pa'i tshul la 'jug par 'gyur ba /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul gyi thabs kyi yul la rnam par 
9b2(mdo sde): 'phrul pa bstan pa zhes bgyi ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs de 'khor 'dir bstan du gsol//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la 'di 
9b3(mdo sde): skad ces bka' stsal to//'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi ldem po ngag dgongs te bstan pa ni lha dang bcas 
9b4(mdo sde): pa'i 'jig rten gyis mos par dka' ba/shes par dka' ba/rtog par dka' ba/rnam par shes par dka' ba/'jug par bka' ba/yongs su rtogs par 
9b5(mdo sde): dka' ba yin pas/cho ga nyams pa 'am/lta ba nyams pa 'am/tshul khrims nyams pas yongs su shes pa 'am//rnam par shes par mi nus so//
9b6(mdo sde): dman pa la mos pa rnams kyis mos par mi nus so//bsam pa nyams pa rnams kyis yid ches par mi nus so//sdig pa'i grogs pos yongs su zin pa rnams kyis 
9b7(mdo sde): 'jug par mi nus so//dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyis yongs su ma zin pa rnams kyis rtogs par mi nus so//chos de lta bu ni yang dag par rdzogs pa'i  sangs rgyas kyis byin 
9b8(mdo sde): gyis ma brlabs bar mnyan bar yang mi nus sa na/mos par lta ga la nus te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs ma gtogs par de ni gnas med do//de nas bcom ldan 
10a1(mdo sde): @#//'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//yid 'ongs snyan ba'i dbyangs dang ldan khyod kyis//nga la gang dris don de 
10a2(mdo sde): ngas bstan nyon//sems can dman pa mun pas bsgribs pa yis//chos tshul dam pa khong du chud mi nus//gang dag sngon sangs rgyas la spyod spyad cing//'jig rten 
10a3(mdo sde): mgon po rnams kyis byin brlabs pa//de dag chos tshul dam pa 'di thos nas/mi rtog shin tu gya nom 'byung bar 'gyur /gang dag sdig grogs sbrul gyis sems zin cing//
10a4(mdo sde): dge ba'i bshes kyi dug sman rnam spangs pa//de dag dam chos dkon mchog 'di thos na/kye ma bag med g.yang sa che las ltung//gang dag sems chung dman 
10a5(mdo sde): zhing zhum gyur dang//gang dag lhag pa'i bsam pa che med pa//yid ched med pa mos pa nyams pas na//thos kyang de dag dga' bar mi 'gyur ro//chos mchog 'di la dad 
10a6(mdo sde): pa ma 'gyur pa//de dag yun ring sdug bsngal ltung 'gyur te//kye ma sdug bsngal rnams la gnod 'gyur zhes//de la snying rje mngon byas de ma bstan //de skad ces dka' 
10a7(mdo sde): stsal pa dang/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das 'khor 'dus pa 'di ni dag pa lags te/spyad pa rnams 
10a8(mdo sde): legs par spyad pa/sangs rgyas byung ba rnams legs par bsnyen bkur bgyis pa dge ba'i bshes gnyen rnams kyis legs par yongs su bzung ba/dad pa'i mig legs par yongs su 
10b1(mdo sde): dag pa/mos pa'i spyod yul la nges par 'byung ba bsam pa dang /lhag pa'i bsam pa legs par yongs su dag pa/rnam pa bzang po rtogs par 'gyur ba sha stag ste/
10b2(mdo sde): 'khor 'dus pa 'di ni de lta bu lags pas/de dag gis chos kyi tshul 'di 'tshal bar 'gyur zhing/rnam par 'tshal bar 'gyur na/sems can rnams la thugs rtse ba'i 
10b3(mdo sde): slad du/bcom ldan 'das kyis legs par yang dag par bshad du gsol //de tshe tshigasu bcad 'di dag kyang gsol to//drang song chen po gang dag chos 'di 'tshal 
10b4(mdo sde): 'gyur ba'i //sems can dpal yon ldan ba 'di na mang du mchis//gang dag sngon sangs rgyas la bcad pa legs spyad pa//de dag rnams la thub dbang de ring chos 
10b5(mdo sde): bstan stsol//de dag gus dang bcas shing thal mo sbyar mchis nas//mi yi lha yi zhal du lta zhing mchis lags na//drang song chen po de slad 
10b6(mdo sde): thugs rje bskyed mdzad cing//dam chos char pa dgyes pa bzhin du dbab du gsol//chos rgyal khyod la bdag ni gsol ba 'debs lags na//chos kyi dam pa 
10b7(mdo sde): gya nom rab tu bstan du gsol//rdzogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' khyu mchog bsdu slad du//mgon po dam chos phyag mdzod dbye ba mdzad du gsol//de skad ces 
10b8(mdo sde): gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//'jam dpal legs so legs so//'jam dpal khyod de 
11a1(mdo sde): @#//bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la don 'di 'dri bar sems pa legs so legs so//'di ltar 'jam dpal 
11a2(mdo sde): khyod ni chos 'di dag la mngon sum du gnas pa/chos thams cad la the tsom med cing/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang thabs la mkhas pas nges par byung ba ste/
11a3(mdo sde): 'jam dpal khyod ni skye bo mang po la phan pa'i phyir zhugs pa'o//'jam dpal khyod ni ma 'ongs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams la snang bar bya bar 'dod pa'o//
11a4(mdo sde): 'jam dpal de'i phyir legs par rab tu nyon la yid la zung shig dang /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul gyi thabs kyi yul la rnam par 'phrul ba 
11a5(mdo sde): bstan pa zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs ngas khyod la bshad do//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin 'tshal lo zhes 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa dang/byang chub 
11a6(mdo sde): sems dpa' de dag bcom ldan 'das kyi ltar nyan pa dang/bcom ldan 'das kyis de dag la 'di skad ces dka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu chos bcu gnyis dang ldan 
11a7(mdo sde): pa'i rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skye bar 'gyur ro//bcu gnyis gang zhe na/'di lta ste/rang bzhin gyis rgya 
11a8(mdo sde): chen po la mos shing dman pa la mos pa med pa yin/rang bzhin gyis snying rje'i spyod yul dang ldan zhing/dkar po'i rang bzhin can yin /bcos ma ma yin pa'i lhag 
11b1(mdo sde): pa'i bsam pa'i spyod yul dang ldan zhing yid dam la bstan pa yin/tshogs shin tu bsags shing spyad pa legs par spyad pa yin/sangs rgyas 'byung ba legs par bsnyen
11b2(mdo sde): bkur cing/dkar po'i chos legs par yongs su bsdus pa yin/lus dang ngag dang yid kyi mtha' kha na ma tho ba med pa dang ldan zhing sdig pa'i las thams cad bor
11b3(mdo sde): ba yin/sdig pa'i grogs po thams cad yongs su spangs shing/dge ba'i bshes gnyen la rten pa yin/ji ltar smras pa/de bzhin du byed cing slu ba med pa yin/
11b4(mdo sde): bged pa'i ngang tshul can yin zhing ro bro ba rnams ma zhen ma chags pa mang ba yin/de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyis byin gyis rlabs shing/bdud kyi byin kyi
11b5(mdo sde): brlabs dang bral ba yin/sems can/thams cad la yang dang yang snying rje chen po yang dag par 'jug pa yin/bdog pa thams cad yongs su
11b6(mdo sde): gtong zhing ma chags pa'i sems dang ldan pa yin te/rigs kyi bu yon tan gyi chos bcu gnyis po de dag dang ldan pa'i rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo bla na
11b7(mdo sde): med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skye ste/'di lta ste/sems can thams cad bde bar bya ba'i phyir phan pa'i sems so//sdig pa thams cad mi
11b8(mdo sde): byed pa'i phyir snying rje'i sems so//sems can thams cad khur bkur ba'i phyir snying rje chen po'i sems so//ngan 'gro thams cad rab tu sim par byed pa'i phyir
11b9(mdo sde): byams pa chen po'i sems so//theg pa gzhan mi 'dod pa'i phyir dag pa'i sems so//nyon mongs pa'i rnyog pa sel ba'i phyir nyon mongs pa med pa'i semaso//
12a1(mdo sde): @#//rang bzhin gyis 'do gsal ba'i phyir 'od gsal ba'i sems so//rdzas med pa'i phyir sgyu ma lta bu'i semaso//dngos po dang bral ba'i phyir dngos po med ba'i 
12a2(mdo sde): sems so//mi sgul ba'i phyir brtan pa'i sems so //chos thams cad nges par rtogs pa'i phyir mi zlog pa'i sems so//ji skad smras pa/de bzhin du byed 
12a3(mdo sde): pa'i phyir sems can thams cad sgrol ba'i sems so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//rgyal sras gang dag spyod 
12a4(mdo sde): pa legs spyad cing//dkar po'i chos kyis bsam pa yongs dag la//dgra la 'ang byams shing snying rjes sems brlan pas//de dag byang chub don 
12a5(mdo sde): tu sems kyang skye//gang dag sdig grogs dgra bo rnam spangs shing//byang chub ston pa'i dge bshes bsten gyur te//byang chub theg par smon lam sbyong 
12a6(mdo sde): byed pa//de dag byang chub donatu sems kyang skye//gang dang sngon mthar spyad pa (sbyor ba )na//nams kyang skyo ba'i sems ni mi skye la//rdo rje lta bur mi zlog sems ldan 
12a7(mdo sde): pa༐ ༐de dag byang chub don tu sems kyang skye//gang dad skye bo kun la byams sems shing//sems cams thams cad bde la sbyor 'dod cing//sdig pa thams cad 
12a8(mdo sde): spong ba'i bsam ba can//de dag byang chub donadu sems kyang skye//theg pa gzhan gyi yon tan mi 'dod cing//byang chub rgya chen yon tan mchog sems shing//rnam 
12b1(mdo sde): dag nyon mongs med pa'i sems ldan pa//de dag byang chub don tu sems kyang skye//srid pa zhi ba gang la yang dag tu//skad cig tsam yang yod med rtog med cing/me long 
12b2(mdo sde): gdong ltar 'gro ba kun lta ba//'di ni yang dag tu na byang chub sems//nyon mongs skyon gyi rdul rnams rnam spangs shing//dri med namakha' lta bu rnam mdzes la//tshig 
12b3(mdo sde): lam nges tshig thams cad mthong dka' ba//'di ni bzang po'i byang chub sems shes bya//gzungs dang so so rig dang spyod mchog gi//rtsa ba 'di yin mtshan gyi 'byung 
12b4(mdo sde): gnas te//dpe byad bzang po 'bar ba lus kyi rgyu//sangs rgyas yon tan kun tu rigs su gnas//gleng bzhi'i le'u ste dang pa'o////'jam dpal yon 
12b5(mdo sde): tan gyi chos de lta bu la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' yon tan gyi phan yon bcu gnyis yang dag par mthong ba des/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa 
12b6(mdo sde): la sbyar bar bya'o//bcu gnyis po dag gang zhe na 'di lta ste/byang chub kyi sems kyi lam rton par yang dag par mthong bas/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyi pa la sbyar bar bya ba dang/
12b7(mdo sde): longs spyod chen po yongs su sgrub pa'i lam yin par yang dag par mthongabs sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin la sbyar bar bya ba dang /'dod pa'i rigs su skye bar 'gyur bar 
12b8(mdo sde): yang dag par mthong bas/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pal sbyar bar bya ba dang /ser sna tshar gcod par 'gyur bar yang dag par mthong bas /sbyin pa'i pha rol 
13a1(mdo sde): @#/ O /tu phyin pa la sbyar bar bya ba dang /gtong ba'i rgyun 'phel bar 'gyur bar yang dag par mthong bas/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la sbyar bar bya pa dang/
13a2(mdo sde): yi dgas kyi grong khyer gyi sgo gcod par 'gyur bar yang dag paramathong bas /sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyi pa la sbyar bar bya ba dang/skye bo mang po dang thun mong gi longs spyod 
13a3(mdo sde): rnams las snying po len par 'gyur bar yang dag par mthong bas sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la sbyar bar bya ba dang/yo byad yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur bar yang 
13a4(mdo sde): dag par mthong bas/sbyinapa'i pha rol tu phyin pa Ila sbyar bar bya ba dang/bdog pa thams cad yongs su gtong ba la goms par byas par 'gyur bar 
13a5(mdo sde): yang dag par mthong bas/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu  phyin pa la sbyar bar bya ba dang /dngos po thams cad la 'dzin pa'i sems bdag la med par 
13a6(mdo sde): 'gyur bar yang dag par ma thong bas/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la sbyar bar bya ba dang /bdag gi sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongasu rdzogs par 'gyur bar yang 
13a7(mdo sde): dag par mthong bas/sbyinapa'i pha rol tu phyinapa la sbyar bar bya ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i rjes su bstan pa la 'thun par zhugs par 'gyur bar yang  
13a8(mdo sde): dag par mthong bas/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pha la sbyar bar bya ba ste/rigs kyi bu yon tan gyi phan yon bcu gnyis po de dag yang dag par mthong ba'i byang chub 
13b1(mdo sde): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la sbyar bar bya ste/sbyin pa de yang bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yongasu bsngo bar bya'o//
13b2(mdo sde): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//sbyin pa rgya chen mtshungs med sbyin pas de dag byang chub 'grub 'gyur zhes//rkang lag mgo dang 
13b3(mdo sde): dri med mig dang rkang dang de bzhin rus pa dang//phyi dang nang gi dngos po thams cad ser sna med pa'i blos kyang gtong //phyir zhing phan yon mthong ba rgya chen sems ldan gang du'ang 
13b4(mdo sde): lta ba med//bdag ni longs spyod bzang po rdzogs 'gyur rigs kyi mchog tu skye bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas dri med la ma 'phel yon tan ser sna yongasu spangs par 'gyur//
13b5(mdo sde): dbang dang pha rol phyin la sogs pa rdzogs 'gyur sbyin/'di rgyal bas bsngags//mkhas pa de ltar du mar rtog cing don kun gtong la dga' bar byed//
13b6(mdo sde): rigs kyi bu yon tan gyi chos bcu gnyis po 'di dag mthong ba'i byang chub sems dpas tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la brtson par bya'o//bcu gnyis po dag 
13b7(mdo sde): gang zhe na/'di lta ste/bdag gis byin gyi rlabs phun sum tshogs pa yongs su zin par 'gyur bar mthong bas/tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la brtson par bya ba dang /
13b8(mdo sde): byang chub sems dpa'i lam du zhugs par mthong bas/tshul khrimasakyi pha rol tu phyin pa la rtson par bya ba dang /bdag gi nyon mongs pa'i 'ching ba rnams lhod par 'gyur 
14a1(mdo sde): @#//bar mthong bas /tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la brtson par bya ba dang /bdag ngan 'gro thams cad las yang dag par 'da' bar 'gyur /
14a2(mdo sde): bar mthong bas/tshul khrims kyi pha rol du phyin pa la brtson par bya dang /bdag gis ngan 'gro'i lam thams cad rnam par sbyangs par 'gyur bas/tshul 
14a3(mdo sde): khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pal brtson par bya ba dang/bdag gis lus dang ngag dang /yid kyi las kha na mtho ba med par 'gyur bar mthong bas/tshul khrimams kyi
14a4(mdo sde): pha rol tu phyin pa la brtsonabar bya ba dang/mkhas pa'i skye bo rnams kyis smad pa med par 'gyur bar mthod bas/tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la brtson pa bya ba dang/bdag 
14a5(mdo sde): bag yod pa'i chos la bsten par 'gyur bar mthong bas/tshul khrims kyi pha rolatu phyin pa la brtson par bya ba dang/bdag gis sems can thams cad la mi
14a6(mdo sde): 'jigs pa'i sbyin pa byin bar 'gyur bar mthong bas /tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la brtson bar bya ba dang /lus dang ngag dang yid kyi las sdoms pa thob par 'gyur bar /
14a7(mdo sde): mthong bas/tshul khrims kyi pha rol du phyin pa la brtson bar bya ba dang/bdag gis sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad bsgrubs par 'gyur bar mthong bas/tshul khrims kyi pha rol
14a8(mdo sde): tu phyin pa la brtson par bya ba dang /bdag gis de bzhin gshegs pa'i tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bla na mang ba yongs su sbyangs par 'gyur bar mthong  s/tshul khrims 
14b1(mdo sde): kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la brtson bar bya ba ste/rigs kyi bu yon tan gyi phan yon bcu gnyis po/de dag yang dag par mthong ba'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos /tshul 
14b2(mdo sde): khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la rtson par bya ste/tshul khrims de yang thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su bsngo bar bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad 
14b3(mdo sde): pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//bdag gi nyon mongs 'ching ba lhod 'gyur zhing /bdag ni ngan 'gro'i sgo gcod 'gyur ro zhes/mkhas pas phan yon yongs su bsam byas la//tshul 
14b4(mdo sde): khrims g.yag rnga bzhin du bsrung bar bya//sangs rgyas rjes bstan rjes su bdag 'brang zhing /bde bar gshegs pas rtag tu bsngags 'gyur la//bdag gis byin 
14b5(mdo sde): brlabs don med mi 'gyur ba//tshul khrims gnas las 'gyur zhes tshul khrims bsrung //lus dang ngag la kha na ma tho med//sems la skyon med chos la 
14b6(mdo sde): spyod par 'gyur//lus kyis bsrungs la ngag gis bsrungs gyur cing //sems kyis sdig pa rnam par sems mi 'gyur//bag yod bde bar gshegs pas bsngags pa ste//
14b7(mdo sde): dge ba'i las kyi rtsa bar gang gyur pa//de la rtag tu bag yod bdag gnas bya//bag med thams cad rtag par yongs su spang //tshul khrims pha rol phyin pa'i mchog gnas 
14b8(mdo sde): na//sangs rgyas chos rnams bdag gis thos par 'gyur//sangs rgyas tshul khrims rab tu sbyang bar bya//de ni tshul khrims mchog ces 'phags pas gsungs//yon tan 
15a1(mdo sde): @#//de dag thob par gang 'dod cing //byang chub sems dpa' byang chub mchog 'dod pa//des ni tshul khrims g.yag rnga bzhin bsrungs na//yon tan thams cad mkhas
15a2(mdo sde): par myur du 'thob //rigs kyi bu gzhan yang chos de lta bu dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos//rnam pa bcu gnyis kyis bzod pa bsgoms par bya'o//
15a3(mdo sde): bcu gnyis po dag gang zhe na/'di lta ste/'du byed thams cad skad cig pa'o zhes bzod pa bsgom par bya ba dang /bdag mi dmigs par bya ba'i phyir bzod pa bsgom par
15a4(mdo sde): bya ba dang /bdag dang gzhan yongs su bsrungs ba'i phyir bzod pa bsgoms par ba ba dang /'khrug pa gtan med par bya ba'i phyir bzod ba bsgom par bya ba dang /bag
15a5(mdo sde): la nyal ba dang /kun nas bkris pa rnam par bsal ba'i phyir bzod pa bsgos par bya ba dang /rjes su chags pa dang /khong khro ba spang pa'i phyir bzod pa bsgom
15a6(mdo sde): par bya ba dang /mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po yongs su bsgrubs pa'i phyir bzod pa bsgoms bar bya ba dang /tshangs pa'i 'jig rten du skye ba thob par bya ba'i phyir bzodapa bsgom
15a7(mdo sde): par bya ba dang/gzhan gyi gnod pa thams cad las yang dag par 'da' bar bya ba'i phyir bzod pa sgom par bya ba dang /zad pa shes pa dang /mi skye ba shes pa thob par bya
15a8(mdo sde): ba'i phyir bzod pa bsgomasapar bya ba dang /bdud kyi yul thams cad las yang dag bar 'da' bar bya ba'i phyir gzod pa bsgom par bya ba dang/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku
15b1(mdo sde): tshad med pa yang dag par mthong bas bzod pa bsgom par bya ba ste/rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos/rnam pa bcu gnyis po de dag gis bzod 
15b2(mdo sde): bsgom par bya ba ste/bzod pa de yang thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su bsngo bar bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag bka' 
15b3(mdo sde): stsal to//kye ma chos rnams skad cig ya ma bla//de la nams kyang sems can rnyed mi 'gyur//yon tan chos rnams rab tu rtogs gyur na/'phags pa sangs rgyas 
15b4(mdo sde): yon tan gnas par 'gyur//mtha' gnyis rnam par spang ba byas pas na//gdag dang gzhan la rtag tu gnod med par//bzod pa phun sum tshogs pa myur bsgom 
15b5(mdo sde): zhes//thugs rje chen po'i dbang po de skad gsungs//rtag tu rnam rtog med par bya ba la//de ni brtan pa'i yid kyis bzod pa bsten//zad pa shes 
15b6(mdo sde): pa bdag gis thob bya zhes//nyon mongs zhi bar bya phyir bzod pa bsten//mtshan gzugs dang byad bzang po dag bya dang//tshangs pa'i gnas su 'gro bar 
15b7(mdo sde): su 'dod pa//brtson 'grus brtan pa de yis bzod stobs sten//bzod la mos na thams cad 'byor bar 'gyur//bdud kyi ltobs ni stobs med bya ba la//bzod pa 
15b8(mdo sde): 'dra ba stobs zla gang yang med//de ni yon tan kun gyi 'byung gnas te/de phyir bzod pa'i yon tan de bstan bya//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang byang chub sems dpas 
16a1(mdo sde): @#//brtul ba bcu gnyis kyis brtson 'grus brtsams bar bya'o//bcu gnyis po dag gang zhe na//'di lta ste/chos thams cad mngon par rdzogs par byang chub 
16a2(mdo sde): bar bya ba'i rtul pas/brtson 'grus brtsam pa bya ba dang/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi drung du 'gro ba'i rtul bas/brtson 'grus brtsam bar bya ba dang /de bzhin gshegs
16a3(mdo sde): pa thams cad mchod cing rim gror bya ba'i rtul bas brtson 'grus brtsams par bya ba dang /de bzhin bshegs pa thams cad mnyes par bya ba'i rtul bas brtsona'agrus 
16a4(mdo sde): brtsam par bya ba dang /sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba'i rtul pas brtson 'grus brtsam bar bya pa dang/sems can thams cad sangs rgyas kyi chos la 
16a5(mdo sde): dgod pa'i rtul pas//brtson 'grus brtsam bar/bya ba dang /sems can thabs cad kyi mi shes pa basla ba'i rtul pas/brtson 'grus brtsam bar ba ba 
16a6(mdo sde): dang/sems can thams chad la sangs rgyas kyi ye shes nye bar bsgrub ba'i rtul pas /brtson 'grus brtsam par bya ba dang/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad yongasu 
16a7(mdo sde): sbyong ba'i rtul pas/brtson 'grus brtsam par ba ba dang /phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i bskal par sems can gyi don tu spyod pa spyad pas yongs su mi skyo ba'i 
16a8(mdo sde): brtul pas/brtson 'grus par bya ba dang /skad cig gcig gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du 'gro ba'i rtul pas/brtsona'agrus brtsam bar bya ba dang /
16b1(mdo sde): sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya zhing/chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba'i rtul bas/brtson 'grus
16b2(mdo sde): brtsam bar bya ba ste/rigs kyi bu rtul pa bcu gnyis po de dag gis/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos brtson 'grus brtsam par bya ste/brtson 'grus de yang 
16b3(mdo sde): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongasu bsngo bar bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//drang srong sras po gang dag byang 
16b4(mdo sde): chub mchog 'dod pa/khyu mchog de dag rnams kyi brtson 'grus ma zhum mchog /de dag sangs rgyas zhing ni brgyar yang 'gro bar byed//de dag yun ring spyad 
16b5(mdo sde): pa spyod kyang skyo ba med//sems can yongs su smin par bya ba'i brtson 'grus can//de dag bskal pa brgya phrag mang por sdug bsngal bzod //skye bo 
16b6(mdo sde): sdug bsngal dpag med zhing bde sbyin 'dod pa//le lo med pa de dag brtson 'grus mi nyams 'dod//zhing kun rnam par sbyong bar bdag ni smon lam 'debs//chos rnam+sa 
16b7(mdo sde): 'di dag thams cad rnam pa kun rtog bya//zhing brgya kun tu chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bar bya//theg pa mchog la bdag gis sems can bye ba gdul//shin tu rgya che zhing 
16b8(mdo sde): rnams sprul pa byas nas su//sems kyi skad cig gcig la byang chub mchog 'tshang //rgyal ba'i sras po brtson 'grus pha rol dong pa dag/sems can don tu rnam par 'phrul 
17a1(mdo sde): @#//ba 'di na ston //rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' rnam pa bcu gnyis kyis bsam gtan rnams bsgom par bya'o//bcu gnyis po dag gang zhe na/'di 
17a2(mdo sde): lta ste/shin tu mi 'khrug pa'i phyir/nyon mongs pa zhi bar bya ba'i rnam pa dang/yul rnams la mi rgyug pa'i phyir/sems gnas pa'i rnam pa dang/'dod pa dang gzugs dang/
17a3(mdo sde): gzugs med pa'i khams las yang dag par 'da' ba'i phyir mi gnas pa'i rnam pa dang /'jig rten pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa zil gyis gnon pa'i phyir/'jig rten las 'das 
17a4(mdo sde): pa'i rnam pa dang/de la ma chags pa'i phyir khyad par gyi rnam pa dang /bsam gtan zlog pa'i phyir 'dod rgyal gyi rnam pa dang/dngos po'i 'du shes dang bral ba'i phyir ngo 
17a5(mdo sde): bo nyid kyi rnam pa dang /tshad yod pa'i ting nge 'dzin la smod pa'i phyir tshad med pa'i rnam pa dang/shin tu zhi ba'i phyir zhi gnas kyi rnam pa dang/cang shes pa'i 
17a6(mdo sde): phyir sems dul pa'i rnam pa dang/dbang po nges par bsdams pa'i phyir nges par sems pa'i rnam pa dang /byang chub kyi bsam pa ma bor ba'i phyir//thabs mkhas 
17a7(mdo sde): pa'i rnam pas byang chub sems dpa' rnams bsam gtan sgom gyi/nga rgyal dang lta ba dang sred pa'i shas che ba'i bsam gtan pa ni ma yin te/de'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' rnam+sa 
17a8(mdo sde): snyoms par 'jug pa thams cad zil gyis gnon to//rigs kyi bu rnam pa bcu gnyis po de dag gis byang chub sems dpas bsam gtan rnams bsgom par bya ste/bsam gtan 
17b1(mdo sde): de yang thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su bsngo bar bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//byang chub sems dpa' gzi ldan 
17b2(mdo sde): pa|che nyid de dag bsam gtan ni//nyon mongs thams cad zhi phyir dang/shin tu 'khrug med don phyir yin//zhi ba'i zhi gnas nges par sems/yul la 'ang rtags par dga' ba med/
17b3(mdo sde): sems 'khrul gyur pa gnas rnamasu//rtag par yang dag rab tu sbyor//mi gnas de dag bsam gtan byed//'dod dang gzugs dang gzugs med pa//gang la 'ang de dag mi gnas
17b4(mdo sde): te||de dag mi gnas bsam gtan byed//byang chub sems dpa' ye shes can//bsam gtan 'jig rten 'a das par brjod//de bas ting nge 'dzin rnams kyis//
17b5(mdo sde): nyan thos rnams dang 'jig rten 'phags//de dag bsam gtan las su rung//ji ltar 'dod bzhin dbang yang sgyur//'dod pa'i khams su skye mi 'dod//de dag
17b6(mdo sde): bsam gtan zlog par byed//bdag nyid chen po de dag gi/bsam gtan rnams ni rnam pas 'phags//de dag der chags med pa zhes//de phyir rgyal bas bka' stsal to//
17b7(mdo sde): sems can med chos dri med de//de dag bsam gtan tshad med do//phyogs gcig bsam gtan byed pa dag/zil gyis mnan cing bsam gtan byed//shes rab thabs dang
17b8(mdo sde): ldan pa po//ma smad bsam gtan bsam gtan byed//de dag lta ba'i rnam pa spangs//shin tu dge ba'i spyod yul can//dngos la mi gnas shes rab ldan//dngos po med
18a1(mdo sde): @#//la 'ang mi gnas te//dngos dang dngos med 'du shes spangs//de dag bsam gtan bsam gtan byed//gzi ldan de dag de 'dra ba'i//
18a2(mdo sde): rnam pas mnyam med bsam gtan byed//de la dgra bcom rang sangs rgyas//blo ni shin tu rmongs par 'gyur//rigs kyi bu rnam pa bcu gnyis kyis byang chub sems 
18a3(mdo sde): dpas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rtogs par bya'o//bcu gnyis po dag gang zhe na/'di lta ste/gti mug gi mun pa mun nag med pa'i phyir snang ba'i rnam pa dang //
18a4(mdo sde): nyon mongs pa'i thibs po snang bar byed pa'i phyir//sgron ma'i rnam pa dang /mi shes pa dang bral ba'i phyir ye shes kyi 'od zer rab tu 'gyed pa'i rnam pa dang /
18a5(mdo sde): ma rig pa drungs nas 'byin pa'i phyir thong gshol gyi rnam pa dang /srid pa'i rgya mo rnam par 'jig par byed pa'i phyir lcags kyo'i rnam pa dang /nyon mongs pa'i 
18a6(mdo sde): ri rdul du rlog pa'i phyir rdo rje'i rnam pa dang /nyon mong pa'i 'dam rdza ba skems pa'i phyir//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gyi rnam pa dang /skye ba'i sheng sreg pa'i phyir me'i rnam 
18a7(mdo sde): pa dang //rmongs pa med par byed pa'i phyir nor bu lta bu'i rnam pa dang /rdzas med pa'i phyir stong pa nyid kyi rnam pa dang /mtshan nyid med pa'i phyir mtshan ma med pa'i rnam 
18a8(mdo sde): pa dang //khams gsum pa las yang dag par 'da' ba'i phyir smon pa med pa'i rnam pa te/rigs kyi bu rnam pa bcu gnyis po de dag gis byang chub sems dpas 
18b1(mdo sde): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rtogs par bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//shes rab 'phags pa 'di ni snang 
18b2(mdo sde): lta bur//rtag tu skye dgu rnams kyi mun pa sel//de ni sgron ma lta bur snang phyir byung//nyon mongs tshogs la 'di ni snang bar byed//sred dang rmongs pa'i rtswa rnam+sa 
18b3(mdo sde): bsal ba'i phyir //dge ba 'di ni thong gshol 'dra ba yin//nyon mongs ri rnams rdul du klog pa ste//lha min sdang la dbang po'i rdo rje bzhin//rang gi snang bas 
18b4(mdo sde): shes rab mun bral bas//dam pa rnams kyis mar me 'dra zhes bstan//de ni nyon mongs 'dam rdzab ngan skems phyir//dpyid nyid 'dra zhes dam pa rnams kyis brjod//de ni nyon mongs chu bor 
18b5(mdo sde): nub pa na//gru gzhin 'jig rten yang dag sgrol ba byed//de ni gti mug shing chen gcod pas na//dag cing mi rtog ral gri rno dang mtshungs//rmongs pa med gyur nor bu rin chen 
18b6(mdo sde): 'dra||dngos med chos kyi mtshan nyid rtogs par byed//stong pa rang dbang med cing rtog med pas//de ni srid dang zhi la mi gnas so//nem nur the tsom la sogs 'jigs byed de//
18b7(mdo sde): dris pa lan gdab pa la 'ang de mkhas so//'khor ba'i nyes pa 'ang de nyid sel byed de//mya ngan 'das lam mtshon la de sgron yin//de la mig ces thub dbang zla ba gsung//mngon sum 
18b8(mdo sde): byed cing rtog pa'i mtshan nyid de//de 'grogs byang chub sems dpa' dri med rnams//sgron ma yod ltar byang chub spyad pa spyod////pha rol tu phyin pa bstan pa'i le'u 
19a1(mdo sde): @#//ste gnyis pa'o////rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa'i thabs ni yul bcu gnyis la blta bar bya ste/bcu gnyis po dag gang zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang 
19a2(mdo sde): chub sems dpa' ni/mya ngan las 'das pa'i yul la gnas pa 'ang yin la//thabs kyis 'khor ba'i yul la 'ang gnas par snang ba dang /dben pa'i yul la gnas pa 
19a3(mdo sde): yang yin la/thabs kyis 'du 'dzi'i yul la gnas par yang snang ba dang /bsam gtan gyi yul la gnas pa 'ang yin la/thabs kyis btsun mo'i 'khor gyi nang 
19a4(mdo sde): na gnas par yang snang ba dang /mngon par 'du mi byed pa'i yul la gnas pa 'angayin la/thabs kyi mngon par 'du byed pa thams cad kyi yul la 
19a5(mdo sde): gnas par yang snang ba dang/skye ba med pa'i yul la gnas pa 'ing yin la/thabs kyis skye ba dang shi 'pho'i yul la gnas par yang snang ba dang /
19a6(mdo sde): bdud bzhi'i yul las yang dag par 'das pa 'ang yin la/thabasakyis bdud 'dul ba yang ston pa dang/'phags pa'i skye bo'i yul la gnas pa 'ang yin la/
19a7(mdo sde): thabs kyis 'phags pa ma yin ba'i yul la gnas par yang sang ba dang/'jig rten gyi yul las yang dag par 'das pa yang yin la/thabs kyisa'ajig 
19a8(mdo sde): rten gyi yul la gnas par yang dang snang ba dang/ye shes kyi yul rnyed pa yang yin la/thabasakyis mi shes pa'i mtha'i yul la gnas par yangasnang ba dang/yang dag pI 
19b1(mdo sde): mtha'i yul mngon sum du gyur pa yang yin la/thabs kyis nyan thos dang/rang sangs rgyas kyi yul du 'ang mi ltung ba dang/mtshan nyid med pa'i chos khong du chud pa yang 
19b2(mdo sde): yin la/thabs kyis mtshan dang dbye byad bzang pos legs par brgyan cing/spud pa'i lus kyi(sa) sems can thams cad yongasu smin par byed pa'i yul la gnas par yang snang ba 
19b3(mdo sde): dang|sangs rgyas kyi yul la yang dag par zhugs pa yang yin la/thabs gyi bdud kyi yul thams cad la gnas par yang snang (ba) ste/rigs kyi bu byang chub sems pa 
19b4(mdo sde): rnams kyis thabs kyi yul bcu gnyis po de dag la lta bar bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//byang chub sems dpa' dag 
19b5(mdo sde): rnams kyi/thabs kyi yul ni 'di yin te//yul rnams gang la gnas gyur kyang//yul rnams kun las snang ba yin//mya ngan 'das pa'i mtshor bkrus kyang//
19b6(mdo sde): thabs kyis 'khor ba'i chu na snang//thabs kyis bya ba thob pas na//gnyi ga'i skyon gyis gos pa med//'gro phan thams cad lus yid kyis//dben pa dam pa thob gyur 
19b7(mdo sde): nas||me tog nags kyi bung ba bzhin//'du 'dzi dag dang 'dres pa med//gdu bu 'khol ba'i sgra chen 'byung ba yi//btsun mo'i 'khor na de dag sgeg par snang//sems 
19b8(mdo sde): can dmyal la sogs pa'i gnod pa yis//sems can sdug bsngal sems shing rtag mi g.yo//de dag rtag tu zhi zhing mnyam bzhag kyang//rnam par g.yengs pa'i yul du 
20a1(mdo sde): @#//'ang snang ba yin//rnam par g.yeng ba'i gzhi ni bsal ba ste//de dag thabs mkhas tshul ni de yin no//de dag mi rtog mngon par 'du byed med//
20a2(mdo sde): rnam par rtog pa'i me yang yod min kyang//thabs kyis spros pa dang ni 'du byed dang//rtog dang ldan pa de dag snang ba yin//chos rnams 'chi 'pho skye ba med ldan zhing//yang 
20a3(mdo sde): dag nyid du skye med ldan mthong yang//mkhas pa rnams kyis thabs kyi ye shes kyis//skye ba dang ni 'chi 'pho ston par byed //bdud kyi yul las yongs 'das shing//brtan 
20a4(mdo sde): rnams sangs rgyas yul rnams kyang/brtan pa rnams kyis thabs kyis su//bdud kyi yul la rgyu bar snang//'phags pa'i tshogs kyi spyir gnas kyang//thabs kyis 
20a5(mdo sde): byin pa nyid du ston//ye shes stobs can rnams 'phrul kyang//sems can dag pa snying rjes zin//chos rnams stong pa nyid kyi mchog/chos nyid de dag rtogs 
20a6(mdo sde): yin zhing//shin tu mya ngan 'das pa yi//thabs la 'ang de dag dpyod par byed//chos kun rang bzhin med pa dang//stong zhi mtshan nyid med rtogs kyang//thabs kyis mtshan 
20a7(mdo sde): gyis brgyan pa yis//lus kyis sems can don yang spyod//shes rab nyams shing brtse med pa//snying rje med blo 'tsho ba min//shes rab 'phags dang snying rje ni//dag 
20a8(mdo sde): pas de phyir thabasu 'dod //byang chub sems dpa' che rnams kyi//mthu ldan thabs ni 'di yin te//'phags pa de dag der gnas shing//rnam par 'phrul ba brgya dag sngon//rigs
20b1(mdo sde): kyi bu 'di ltar yang de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi thabs su blta bar bya ste/gang 'di ltar yon tan rnams kyi snying po rnam pa bcu gnyis kyis mngon par 'phags pa'i sangs
20b2(mdo sde): rgyas kyi zhing du/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas kyang//bskal pa'i snyigs ma ston pa dang /dus kyi snyigs ma ston pa dang/
20b3(mdo sde): sems can gyi snyigs ma ston pa dang//nyon mongs pa'i snyigs ma ston pa dang/tshe'i snyigs ma ston pa dangtheg pa tha dad pa ston pa dang/kun nas nyon mongs pa can gyi sangs 
20b4(mdo sde): rgyas kyi zhing ston pa dang/sems can dmu rgod ston pa dang/chos bstan pa la bye brag ston pa dang/gzhan mu steg can gyi 'khrug pa yod par ston pa dang/bdud 
20b5(mdo sde): rnams dang/bdud kyi las rnams kyang ston la//nyes pa de dag kyang yod pa ma yin pa thams cad ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i thabasu blta bar bya'o//de skad ces 
20b6(mdo sde): bka' stsal pa dang/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//yon tan gyi snying po gang dang ldan na/gang du sangs rgyas bcom 
20b7(mdo sde): ldan 'das rnams byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya ba'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing de lta bur gyur pa'i yon tan gyi snying po bcu gnyis po de dag bcom ldan 'das kyis 
20b8(mdo sde): bstan du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' stsal pa/'jam dpal 'di la gang du sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshangargya ba'i 
21a1(mdo sde): @#//sangs rgyas kyi zhing de ni yon tan gyi snying pos bsgrubs pa'i phyir bskal pa'i snying po dang ldan pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi zhing de ni chos spyad pa'i dus las mi 
21a2(mdo sde): 'da' ba'i phyir/dus kyi snying po dang ldan pa yin /sangs rgyas kyi zhing de nichos shes par 'gyur ba dang ldan pa'i phyir/sems can gyi snying pa dang ldan pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi zhing 
21a3(mdo sde): de ni legs par yongs su dag pa'i phyir/nyon mongs pa can ma yin pa'i/zhing gi snying po dang ldan pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi zhing de na sems can dmu rgod med pa'i phyir/sems 
21a4(mdo sde): can cang shes kyi snying po dang ldan pa yan/sangs rgyas kyi zhing de de ni theg pa gcig gis nges par 'byung ba'i phyir/theg pa chen po dang ldan pa yin/sang rgyas kyi zhing de 
21a5(mdo sde): ni rdzas kyi lhag ma yod pa'i phyir/sa'i snying po dang ldan pa yin/sangs rgya kyi zhing de ni gzhan mu stegs can thams cad kyi bstan pa mi spyod pa'i phyir /
21a6(mdo sde): bstan pa'i snying po dang ldan pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi zhing deni g.yo med pa'i phyir/yon tan gyi snying po dang ldan pa yinsangs rgyas kyi zhing de ni sems can rang bzhin gyis dkar ba'i 
21a7(mdo sde): gnasu gyur pa'i phyir/bsam pa dang lhag pa'i bsam pa'i snying po dang ldan pa yani/sangs rgyas kyi zhing de nisbyin pa'i gnas dang mi 'bral ba'i phyir/'phags pa'i snying po 
21a8(mdo sde): dang ldan ba yin/sangs rgyas kyi zhing do ni sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams bzhud cing rjes su bzhud pa'i phyir/byang chub kyi snying po dang ldan pa yin te/'jam dpal gang sangs 
21b1(mdo sde): rgyas bcom ldan 'das byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya ba'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing de ni/yon tan gyi snying po de dag dang ldan no//thabs la mkhas pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////
21b2(mdo sde): 'jam dpal de ltar rnam grangs des kyang 'di ltar nga la nyan thos dang rang sang+yas kyi theg pa rnam par dgod pa med par khyod kyis rig par bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de bzhin gshegs pa 
21b3(mdo sde): la 'du shes tha dad pa med pa'i phyir ro//'jam dpal gal te de bzhin gshegs pa sems can kha cig la ni theg pa chen po ston//kha cig la ni rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa ston/
21b4(mdo sde): kha cig la ni nyan thos kyi theg pa ston na/de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i sems yongs su ma dag par 'gyur ro//sems ma snyoms pa nyid du yang 'gyur ro//mngon par zhen 
21b5(mdo sde): pa'i skyon du yang 'gyur ro//snying rje chen po phyogs gcig par yang 'gyur ro//'du shes tha dad pa'i skyon du yang 'gyur ro//ngas chos la slob dpon gyi dpe 
21b6(mdo sde): mkhyud byas par yang 'gyur ro/'jam dpal das sems can rnams la chos gang dang gang bstan pa de dag thams cad ni/'di lta ste/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes 
21b7(mdo sde): thob par bya ba'i phyir byang chub la gzhol pa/theg pa chen po la 'bab pa/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid sgrub pa/yul gcig tu yang dag par phyin par byed pa ste/de bas na nga la 
21b8(mdo sde): theg pa tha dad par rnam par dgod pa med do//'jam dpal gyis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das gal te theg pa rnam par dgod pa ma mchis na/ci'i slad du de bzhin gshegs 
22a1(mdo sde): @#//pas 'di ni nyan thos kyi theg pa'o//'di ni rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa'o//'di ni theg pa chen po'o zhes theg pa gsum la brtsams te/sems can rnams la 
22a2(mdo sde): chos bstan pa mdzad lags /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/'jam dpal theg pa rnam par dgod pa zhes bya ba de ni de bzhin gshegs pas sa rnam par dgod pa 
22a3(mdo sde): mdzad pa yin gyi/theg pa rnam par dgod pa ma yin no//de bzhin gshegs pas chos kyi mtshan nyid rnam par dgod pa mdzad pa yin /de bzhin gshegs pas gang 
22a4(mdo sde): zag rnam par dgod pa mdzad yin /de ni de bzhin gshegs pa tshogs chung ba dang/tshogs tshad med pa rnam par dgod pa mdzad ba yin te/
22a5(mdo sde): chos kyi dbyings tha dad pa med pa'i phyir /'di la theg pa tha dad par 'gyur ba gang yang med do//'jam dpal de ni de bzhin gshegs bas 'jug pa'i sgo stan 
22a6(mdo sde): pa ste/gzo slob pa ni rims kyis slob pa la 'jug pa ltar blta bar bya'o//'jam dpal 'di lta ste dper na/'phong gi slob dpon 'phong dpyad kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i mchog
22a7(mdo sde): thob pa ni/slob ma rnams la thabs sna tshogs dang/rtsol ba sna tshogs kyis 'phong dpyad ston te/'phong shes par bya ba ni gcig tu zad do//'jam dpal de bzhin du de bzhin 
22a8(mdo sde): gshegs pa chos kyi 'phong gi slob dpon mkhas pa yang thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes gcig po de nyid rnam pa gsum du ston mdzad do//'jam dpal 'di lta sta dper na/
22b1(mdo sde): me lce chung ngu de nyid mched na /sreg pa'i bskal pa'i bar du 'tshig pa'i mthu yod par 'gyur ro//'jam dpal de bzhin du ye shes snang ba gcig po de yang rim gyis rnam
22b2(mdo sde): par 'phel na/nyon mongs pa thams cad sreg pa'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi snang ba chen po'i bar du sbyin par byed do//'jam dpal 'di lta ste dper na/
22b3(mdo sde): ri'i rgyal po ri rab ni rnam par rtog pa med de /de'i drung du sems can gang dag kha cig phyin pa de dag thams cad 'di lta ste /gser gyi mdog tu kha dog gcig par
22b4(mdo sde): 'gyuro //'jam dpal de bzhin ngu de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shas kyi ri rab chen po bla na med pa yang rnam par mi rtog ste /der sems can gang dag kha cis de bzhin
22b5(mdo sde): gshegs pa'i chos nyid la rnam par lta ba de dag thams cad 'di la ste /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi mdog tu kha dog gcig pa 'gyur ro//'jam
22b6(mdo sde): dpal 'di lta ste dper na /nor bu rin po che chen po an da rnyil ni phyogs gang na 'dug pa der rin po che chen po de'i yul du ji tsam du gyur pa tshun chad na /kha dog tha dad
22b7(mdo sde): pa gzugs kha dog sna tshogs snang ba gang dag ji snyed cig yod pa de dag nor bu rin po che de'i gzi brjid kyis /'di lta ste /sngo ba'i mdog tu kha dog gcig pas 'gyur ro//
22b8(mdo sde): 'jam dpal de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa bla na med pa'i nor bu rin po che an da rnyil chen po'i chos snang ba dang /'od kyis sems can gang la bab pa de thams cad 'di
23a1(mdo sde): @#//lta ste/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi mdog tu kha dog gcig par 'gyur ro//'jam dpal 'di lta ste dper na/rgya mtsho chen por ni sgo tha dad pa nas 
23a2(mdo sde): chu mang po 'bab ste/der bab nas thams cad kyang 'di lta ste/lan tsha'i ror ro gcig par rtag tu gnas par 'gyuro //'jam dpal de la rgya mtsho chen po zhes bya ba de ni 
23a3(mdo sde): de bzhin gshegs pa'i tshig bla dgas so//sgo tha dad pa nas chu mang po 'bab ces bya ba de ni nyan thos dang/rang sangs rgyas dang/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi tshig 
23a4(mdo sde): bla dgas so//der bab nas de dag thams cad ro gcig tu 'gyur zhes bya ba de ni rnam par mi rtog pa'i phyir/theg pa gcig gis tshig bla dgas te/'jam dpal 
23a5(mdo sde): rnam grangs des kyang 'di ltar de bzhin gshegs pas de ni yongs su sbyang ba bya ba'i sar rnam par dgod pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pas 'jug pa'i tshul du gsungs pa/
23a6(mdo sde): de ni de bzhin gshegs pas chos rnam par dgod pa mdzad pa/de ni de bzhin gshegs pasabastan pa la rim gyis 'jug par bstan pa ste/tshogs chu du dang/tshogs chen po'i gang 
23a7(mdo sde): zag rnams rnam par bkod pa/thabs mkhas pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i so so yang dag par rig pa sna tshogs dang/kun rdzob la 'jug par bstan pa yin 
23a8(mdo sde): par de ltar rig par bya ste/don dam par ni theg pa gcig ste gnyis su med do//'jam dpal sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di na gzhan mu stegs can spyod pa pa dang/kun tu rgyu ba dag snang ba 'di yang 
23b1(mdo sde): de bzhin gshegs pa'i thabs yin par blta bar bya ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyis mnga' dbang yin par rig par bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na /'jam dpal de bzhin gash+ye 
23b2(mdo sde): pas ni gnyen po thams cad shin tu bsal ba'i phyir//phyir rgol ba thams cad kyis thams cad med de/'jam dpal 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po dge ba'i rtsa ba 'jig pa'i tshul 
23b3(mdo sde): can dang ldan pa/'dod chags dang bcas pa/zhe sdang dang bcas pa/gti mug dang bcas pa /nye bar len pa dang bcas pa/bag la nyal dang bcas pa/nyon mongs pa dang bcas pa la yang phyir 
23b4(mdo sde): rgol ba thams cad kyis thabs cad med cing sngo ba'i dmag tshogs med do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'jam dpal 'khor los/sgyur ba'i rgyal po la 'tshe ba med pa'i phyir te/'jam 
23b5(mdo sde): dpal 'khor los sgyur ba'irgyal po yang de lta na/de bzhin gshegs pa chos kyi 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal pa bsod nams dang /ye shes kyi tshogs tshad med pa bsags 
23b6(mdo sde): pa|snying rje chen po rgyun mi 'chad pa rnyes pa/zag pa med pa'i chos kyi namakha' la rgyu ba/byang chub kyi yan lag bdun gyi rin po che dang ldan pa/brnyel pa mi mnga' ba'i chos can chos kyi 
23b7(mdo sde): 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po chen po'i chos kyi rgyal srid la tshe ba yod pa 'am/sngo ba'i dmag tshogs kyi 'jigs par 'gyur ba lta ga la yod de de ni gnas med do//'jam dpal 
23b8(mdo sde): yang sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di na gzhan mu stegs can spyod pa pa dang /kun tu rgyu ba gang dag snang ba 'di yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs yin par blta bar bya ste/rigs kyi bu de 
24a1(mdo sde): @#//dag kun kyang mu stegs gcig la gnas pa yin par rig par bya'o//'jam dpal 'di lta ste/ri dgas kyi rgyal po seng ge'i mdun du ni/ri dgas phra mo thams
24a2(mdo sde): cad gnas par mi nus so//'jam dpal de bzhin du gzhan mu stegs can spyod pa pa dang/kun tu rgyu thams cad kyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul gnan phar bya ba 'am/de bzhin 
24a3(mdo sde): gshegs pa'i skyes bu seng ge chen po stobs bcu mnga' ba/mi 'jigs pa bzhi brnyed pa'i mdun du gnas par bya ba 'am/brtsad par bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs 
24a4(mdo sde): ma gtogs par mi nus te de ni gnas med do//'jam dpal 'di lta ste dper na /nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor shar cing 'od zer mang po bkye ba'i tshe /srin bu me khyer tham+sa 
24a5(mdo sde): cad kyang 'on med par 'gyur zhing/me dang nor bu dang skar ma thams cad kyi 'od rnams kyang mi snang ngo//'jam dpal de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa nyi ma'i 
24a6(mdo sde): dkyil 'khor chen po shar cing/ya shes kyi 'od zer mang po bkye ba'i tshe/gzhan mu stegs can spyod pa pa dang/kun tu rgyu thams cad zil gyis non cing mi snang ngo//
24a7(mdo sde): 'jam dpal 'di lta ste dper na/lcags kyi rgyal po khab longasa'i phyogs gang du byung ba der khab long gi mi 'dre ba'i mtshan nyid kyi phyir lcags thams cad mi gnas so//'jam 
24a8(mdo sde): dpal de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi zhing gang du sangs rgyas byung bar gyur pa de na/gzhan mu stegs can spyod pa pa dang/kun tu rgyu thams cad rgyu ba med par rig par bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/
24b1(mdo sde): de ni sangs rgyas byung ba'i mi 'dre ba'i chos kyi mtshan nyid kyi phyir ro//'jam dpal 'di lta ste dper na/rin po che'i rgyal po yid bzhin gyi nor bu gang du byung ba der ni 'ching bu rnams mi 
24b2(mdo sde): 'byung ngo//'jam dpal de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa ye shes rin po che chen po gang du byung ba de na gzhan mu stegs can spyod pa pa dang/kun tu rgyu thams cad mi 'byung bar rig 
24b3(mdo sde): par bya'o//'jam dpal 'di lta ste/dper na/rin po che'i 'byung khung gang nas/'dzam bu chu bo'i gser 'byung ba de nas lcags 'gyes kyi phung po mi 'byung ngo//'jam dpal
24b4(mdo sde): de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams gang du de bzhin de gshegs pa byung ba der gzhan mu stegs can spyod pa dang/kun tu rgyu thams cad mi 'byung bar rig par bya'o//'jam dpal rnam 
24b5(mdo sde): grangs des kyang/'di ltar de bzhin gshegs pa gang du byung ba der gzhan mu steg can spyod pa pa dang/kum tu rgyu thams cad mi 'byungabar rig par bya'o//'jag dpal 
24b6(mdo sde): yang sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di na gzhan mu stegs can sbyod pa pa dang/kum tu rgyug gang dag snang ba 'di yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyis blabs dang/thabs la mkhas pa'i yul 
24b7(mdo sde): bsam gyis mi khyab pa yin par rig par bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar mu stegs can gzhan gyi sgo 'di dag thams cad ni rnam par thar pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i sgo la 
24b8(mdo sde): gnas pa dag ste/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa las nges par 'byung ba dag go/thabs la mkhas pas rnam par rtse ba dag go/sangs rgyas dang/chos dang /dge 'dun yid la 
25a1(mdo sde): @#//bya ba ma btang ba dag go/sems can yongs su smin par bya ba rnams la dam pa'i pha rol tu son pa dag go/sems can yongasu smin par bya ba'i phyir/
25a2(mdo sde): de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyis rlabs kyis byin gyis brlabs pa dag go/bstan pa 'di bshad pa na lha'i bu nyan thos kyi theg pa la gnas pa brgyad stong gis theg pa gcig
25a3(mdo sde): ces thos nas /bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed par gyur to//dge slong lnga brgyas theg pa gcig tu yang dag par 'du ba'i sgron ma'i ting nge 
25a4(mdo sde): 'dzin thob par gyur to//byang chub sems dpa' stong nyis brgyas mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob par gyur to//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig 
25a5(mdo sde): rten gyi khams 'di yang rnam pa drug tu g.yos par gyur to//steng gi bar snang las kyang lha'i me tog ud pa la dang/pad+ma dang/ku mu da dang /pad ma dkar 
25a6(mdo sde): po'i char mngon bar bab bo//lha'i tsan dan gyi phye ma'i char dag kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhabs kyi drung du bab par gyur to//lha'i bu 'bum dag kyang steng gi bar snang 
25a7(mdo sde): la 'dug nas/ca co dang ga zhi' sgra brgya stong du ma phyung bar gyur te/lha'i gos dag g.yab mo'i thabasu skyob bo//lha'i rnga dag kyang brdungs par gyur to//ched du brjod pa 
25a8(mdo sde): 'di skad kyang ched du brjod de/kye ma sngon ma thos pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs 'jig rten du byung ngo//bcom ldan 'das chos kyi rnam grangs 'di yun ring por 'dzam bu'i gling na
25b1(mdo sde): gnas par gyur cig ces brjod do//dge slong ma brgyad brgyas kyang rang rang gi bla gos de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku la gsol bar gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyi de'i tshe 
25b2(mdo sde): don 'di nyid rgya cher bstan pa'i phyir tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//nga'i thabs yul bsam gyis mi khyab 'di//'jam dbyangs khyod kyis de ltar shes par 
25b3(mdo sde): gyis//bcud kyi dus la byang chub sangs rgyas kyang //'di 'dra'i dus 'di nga yis ston pa mdzad //nga la dus kyis nyes pa mi mnga' ste//chos kyi dus ni rtag tu nye bar gnas 
25b4(mdo sde): nga las sems can chos thos 'jug par byed//sems can skyon ni nam yang yod ma yin//yon tan rdzas kyi khyad kyis bskal pa'i bcud//dri med yongs su dag pa 
25b5(mdo sde): zhing gi bcud//dus kyi bcud la byang chub ngas brnyes te//de bas nga la rnyog pa yongs med do//ngas ni bskal ba bye ba ji snyed kyis//sangs rgyas ye shes mtshungs 
25b6(mdo sde): med bsgrubs byas pa//byang chub brnyes nas de srid nga yi tshe//bar ma dor ni mya ngan 'da' ba med//'on kyang mya ngan 'da' ba ngas bstan te//rtag pa'i 'dus shes can rnams 
25b7(mdo sde): mi rtag bstan//lhag ma lus pa ltar yang ngas bstan te//nga yi tshe ni phyi ma'i mthar brtags so//nga yi mya ngan 'da' gcig theg pa gcig/nga yis theg pa tha dad med 
25b8(mdo sde): pa ste//yang dag tu na theg pa gcig tu bstan//de ni thabs kyi yul du shes par gyis//rgyud zhan bsam pa chung ba'i skye bo dag//'phral du thos pas 'jigs par 'gyur 
26a1(mdo sde): @#//srid ces//de dag rnams la theg pa gsum bstan gyi//theg pa gcig ste theg pa gnyis su med //ngas bstan 'dis ni chos la mos pa dang//bstan 
26a2(mdo sde): pa'i lam la de bzhin 'jug 'gyur zhes//theg pa gcig po rnam pa gsum du bstan//theg pa tha dad nam yang yod pa min//dper na 'phong shes pha rol phyin gyur pa//shes 
26a3(mdo sde): pa de nyid rnam pa gsum du ston//de bzhin ston pa chos la mkhas pa'i mchog /theg pa gcig po rnam pa gsam du ston//'dren pa sems can kun la mnyam pa'i 
26a4(mdo sde): sems//nga la tha dad 'du shes nam yang med//theg pa dman pa zhan la ngas bstan na//de ni nga la ser sna'i skyon du 'gyur//dper na rin chen dam pa an da rnyil//

26a6(mdo sde): rin chen yang//de bzhin 'od 'gyed zhing kun rgyas mdzad cing//srog chags thams cad kha dog gcig bsgyur te//byang chub mdog bsgyur rnam rtog sems mi mnga'//dper na me stag

26a8(mdo sde): drung ༐ phyin pa de dag de mthus mdog gcig 'gyur//de bzhin stobs bcu nga gan gang 'ongs pa//mkhas pa de dag byang chub mchog tu 'gyur//dper na sbrang ma dag gi mchu yis ni//
26b1(mdo sde): bzang dang mi bzang sna tshogs gang blangs pa//de dag thams cad gcig tu sogs byas shing //sbrang rtsi nyid du sgyur bar byed pa ltar//de bzhin mkhas pa 'jig rten grangs pa yang //

26b3(mdo sde): med de//de la spo ba'i dmag tshogs yod ma yin//chos kyi rgyal srid chen po ngos bzung na//de la mu stegs can gyi yul med do//dper ni nyad ma'i dkyil 'khor shar gyur

26b5(mdo sde): ni gang na khab long gnas gyur pa//de na lcags kyi rnam pa gzhan med do//zhing mchog gang na de bzhina gshegs pa bzhugs//der ni mu stegs can rnams 
26b6(mdo sde): 'byung ba med//gser gyi rgyal po yul gang byung gyur pa//de nas 'gyes pa'i phung po'i lcags mi 'byung //gang du 'dren bas byang chub brnyes gyur pa//zhing der mu

27a1(mdo sde): @#//pas blo ldan rnams//rnam par 'phrul pa sna tshogs ston par byed//thabs kyi yul de thos par gyur pa'i tshe//rgyal ba'i sras rnams

28a1(mdo sde): @#//ma nyams pa dge sbyong dang bram ze'i gan du byon pa legaso//rgyal po chen po khyod kyi rgyal srid la 'tshe ba ma mchis sam/gnod par bgyid pa ma mchis

57a1(mdo sde): @#//bde bar gshegs pa'i yon tan dri med chos //gang lags de kun thub pa nyid gsung grangs//bram ze ci'i phyir na dge sbyong go'u ta me de bsod nams

59a1(mdo sde): @#//phyang nger gnas pa'i phyal dang ldan//'dod dang 'jigs med nag nog med pa'i sku//smin ma gnag cing shin tu bzang ring mdzes//phya la ni ma zhom

62b5(mdo sde): gyur pa'i sems can rnams gzigs //stobs bcu mnga' ba'i thags rje rab tu skye//'khor bar bcings pa'i sems dang ldan pa //mi khom skye ba'i skye dgu 'di 
62b6(mdo sde): dag dang//log par lta bar lhung ba rnams gzigs na//stobasabacu mnga' ba'i thugs rje rang tu skye//sdug bsngal bde dang mi sdug sdug shes dang/mi rtag rtag dang

63a1(mdo sde): @#//pa'i yid ldan dang//sred pa che zhing rtag tu yul bskam dang//sred pa'i 'dod chags rgya mtshor lhung ba rnams//gzigs na rgyal ba'i

63a3(mdo sde): mnga' ba'i thugs rje rab tu skye//thub pa'i thugs rje chen po rtag 'byung ste//de mi 'byung ba nam yang srid mi 'gyur//'gro ba kun gyi bsam bar gnas pa ste//de bas sangs 
63a4(mdo sde): rgyas de la skyon mi mnga' //rgyal po chen po mdor na dge sbyong go'u ta ma de ni dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi dang ldan pa'o//rgyal po chen po dge sbyong go'u ta ma de ni yang 
63a5(mdo sde): dag pa'i spong ba bzhin dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi dang/dbang pa lnga dang/stobs lnga dang/byang chub kyi yan lag bdun dang/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa dang ldan pa ste/

63a8(mdo sde): drang srong chen po rig dang rnam grol mkhas//thub pa rnam dag sems can thabs mkhyen ldan//byams dang snying rje dga' dang btang snyoms dang//tshangs pa'i gnas pa 
63b1(mdo sde): bzhi la de dbang sgyur//lhag mthong zhi gnas gnyis la rnal 'byor pa//de bas de la rnam yang skyon ma mchis//byang chub yan lag rin chen rgyan mchog ldan //yan lag

63b5(mdo sde): gshegs pa ni gnas la yang gnas su yang dag pa/ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu mkhyen/gnas ma lags pa/la yang gnas ma lags par yang dag pa ji lta bzhin du

64a1(mdo sde): tshogs yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu mkhyen to//sems can gzhan dag dang/gang zag gzhan rnams kyi dbang po mchog dang /mchog ma

64a3(mdo sde): gzhan rnams kyi dbang po dang /stobs dang /byang chub kyi yan lag dang /bsam gtan dang /rnam par thar pa dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /snyoms pa re 'jug pa rnams kyi kun

64b1(mdo sde): po dag ste/stobs de dag dang ldan pas/de bzhin gshegs pa de stobs bcu mnga' zhes bgyi ste/de'i slad du de skyon ma mchis lagaso//de la 'di skad ces bgyi ste/

297a6(mdo sde): mo 'dra//de'i sa la bzhugs pa'i lha'i yang lha gzugs ni rnams par mdzes ste//de gzugs lha rgyal go ba ka dag glog dang 'ol ma byu ru 'ong bzhin du//la las bal chung
297a7(mdo sde): sku dang na bza' lo ma rtsa la sogs pa'i mal bzhugs par//mthong nyi ma stong ltar shin tu rnam par 'bar ba'i sku gzugs mnga' ba la//thub can la las rnal 'byor dbang 
297a8(mdo sde): la stug dbus chu skyes re re bzhugs par mthong //thub pa'i thor tshugs tshogs ni lag gis 'gro yi mgo bo klong pa bzhin du ste//la lar gzugs ni rdo an 'da' 
297b1(mdo sde): rnyil gyis spras pa'i cod pan brgyan paro//la las la lar thub pa de la 'khor lo ri mo nya dung bcas par mthong //la las ji ltar namakha' rgyu ba'i lam la 'ja' zug
297b2(mdo sde): bzhin du thub pa mthong //lha rdzas phyag sor can gyis sna tshogs yul can ri rab phyag tu rab gzhog cing //khams sum chu srin gnas dang chu gnas de bzhin ba lang rmig
297b3(mdo sde): par shong par mdzad//sbyin pa'i shugs kyis mi yi bdag po'i gtsug gi cod pan rnams kyis rab mchod cing //la la dag na rgyal dang rgyal po'i 'khor na dgos pa 
297b4(mdo sde): rnams la mkhas gyur cing //chad pas gcod par sno stug po'i dbus na gzhan du rnam par snang ba ni//thub pa rnal 'byor mkhas pa'i ye shes rnal 'byor shin tu 
297b5(mdo sde): byed pa rnams la ste//rang gi yul rnams sngon gyi rgyal bas gsungs pa de dag rnal 'byor gyis ston cing //la las gnas dang lta ba yid dang blo rnams shin tu 
297b6(mdo sde): gzhan du gyur pa dang //dbang dang ye shes rnam pa sna tshogs ting 'dzin dang yid bzhin sku mnga' bar//la las yul la chags med spyod yul rig pa'i blo'i bya ba gcod 
297b7(mdo sde): par te//'di dang gzhan med bsreg med gyur pas tshig bya med phyir mya ngan 'das paro//snum dang 'od 'phro med pas ji ltar 'jig rten 'di na me bzhin shin tu mya ngan 'das//la las
297b8(mdo sde): pha rol phyin pa'i mchod sprin mchod bcu mchod sbyin byed pa dag tu ste//sbyin dang mchod dang thub pa dag kyang rnam pa tshul bzhin mdzad par shin tu mthong //bde ba yod ces 
298a1(mdo sde): @#//bya ba'i zhing mchog de na skyes bu yod pa de dag ni//mngal nas skyes pa ma yin bde bar 'bar ba'i 'od kyi dkyil 'khor can dag ste//lha rdzas gser ltar 'bar 
298a2(mdo sde): dang zla ba lta bu'i lus 'chang rnal 'byor can gyi bdag/lha rdzas 'od bzang de dang gang yang mi nang rnal 'byor can gyi dbang po'i yul//de dag rgya cher mi phod rnal 'byor can 
298a3(mdo sde): gyi dbang po dag gis bde de la//gzhan du bdag cag nyid kyis bshams pa'i longs la spyod pa'i zhing gi me de dag /mtha' yas 'od kyi bde ba can na rgyal ba'i sras rnams 
298a4(mdo sde): lha rdzas 'od 'khor can //rnal 'byor yang dag nyid mos thub dang khyu mchog bsam gtan bdud rtsi myong byed dang /rnam pa tha dad sna tshogs rin po che dag yal ga spyangs
298a5(mdo sde): pa'i nags tshal shing //lha rdzas gser phye'i khams la bsagsa pa'i zhing mchog de ni shin tu rnam par mdzes //bser dang rin chen sna tshogs me tog pad+mos de ni mchog
298a6(mdo sde): tu rab tu brgyan //'di ni sangs rgyas zhing gter yin dpe med de kun ming yang pad mo skyes//thub pa de la dad cing bsam gtan mkhas la sangs rgyas skyid la rab tu mos//bdag 
298a7(mdo sde): gis ston pa la ni bdag bstan yid can bad mo dag las skyes pa ste//sangs rgyas zhing gi mchog na gnas pa'i ming ni bzang po'i mtshan gyis kun nas brgyan//dpe byad bzang 
298a8(mdo sde): po'i bcas pas rmad du 'bar ba'i lus can dpal gyi be'u dang bcas//yod dang med pa'i rnam rtog rtog pa med pas dri med rdzu ba med dag go/de ltar 'di dag rnam par 
298b1(mdo sde): bstan nas rdo+e khyu mchog mthe bo tsam du gyur//sro ma rdul phran shin tu phra ba'i skra yi brgya cha'i gzugs su 'ang de bzhin gyur//la lar bder gshegs rang rgyal nyan thos 
298b2(mdo sde): gzugs dang de bzhin sna tshogs gzhan dag kyang//gang dang gang la sems kyi spyod pa ci bzhin de 'dra'i gzugs kyi khyad par dag/rnal 'byor mkhas pa rdo rje zhes byas mtshan 
298b3(mdo sde): med gzugs kyi yul dag rnam par spangs//de skad bshad nas yang smras pa//sna tshogs gzugs can rgyal ba'i sras//sa rnams de bzhin dbang rnams dang//ma nyam+sa
298b4(mdo sde): mngon bar shes pa dang//sgyu ma lta bu'i ting 'dzin dang //de ltar yid bzhin lus kyang ste//gzugs dang rdzu 'phrul rkang pa dang//rnam thar tha dad yang dag rig /stobs dang 
298b5(mdo sde): 'jigs pa med pa dang//phung po khams rnams zag med pa//'di dag gzhan du gyur nas 'thob//rtag pa'i sangs rgyas sa yang ngo//chos rnams lnga'i rang 
298b6(mdo sde): bzhin dang//rnam par shes pa brgyad rnams dang//bdag med gnyis po rab rtogs nas//gzhan du 'gyur ba 'byung bar 'gyur //yid bzhin lus ni bcu po dag/sgyu ma dpa' 
298b7(mdo sde): bar 'gro bas bskor//mi zad smon lam bcu po dang//lam gyi rnams kyis shin tu sprul//rmi lam me long gzugs brnyan nam//chu yi zla ba bzhin du mthong //rnal 'byor
298b8(mdo sde): lam la thub pa'i rnams//sangs rgyas sa la zhugs pa dang //rgyal ba pad mo'i gdan bzhugs pa//rgyal ba'i sras kyis mdun du byas//yod dang med pa'i phyogs bral ba//
299a1(mdo sde): @#//rdul phran rnam par 'jig rnams kyis//dri za'i grub pa'i rgyal po dang//lha yi gnas na 'khod pa'i tshogs//ri rab sa la 'dod rgyu zhing//namakha'i dkyil 
299a2(mdo sde): nas 'khod spyod kyang//ji ltar snang bar ma gyur pa//de bzhin rgyal sras gces pa'i rnams//rnal 'byor med pas (mi )mthong ngo//rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs dang ldan //sa dang 
299a3(mdo sde): zhing rnams bye ba na//rgyal ba mchog la mngon bar mos//smon lam dbang la gnas pa'i rnams//pad+mo'i khang par rab tu skye//dbang dang bsam gtan sems pa dag/zhing 
299a4(mdo sde): dang zhing du mya ngan 'da'//tinge 'dzin las skyes sa pa ston//lhun gyis grub par thams cadu //chur byung zla ba 'dra bar te//nyi ma lta bur khyad med pa//snang ba sems 
299a5(mdo sde): can spyod yul du//yang dag mtha' la mi reg par//ting nge 'dzin las rab tu ldog/gru la gnas pa'i bung ba dag/gangs chen mtsho la 'gro ba bzhin //gang du gru 'gro 
299a6(mdo sde): der rgyug ste//dpag tshang shin tu mang bar yang//thugs rjes nyon mongs dma' ba'i lus//sdug bsngal can gzugs de bzhin du//de dag 'byung ba rab tu ston //phyir 
299a7(mdo sde): ldog bdag med pa yang ste//'jig pa dag kyang nam mkhyen nas//zad pa'i dus su mi gnas pa//gzungs dang 'dzin gyis 'phangs pa'i rnams//gzung dang 'dzin la spyod pa'i 
299a8(mdo sde): rnams //gzung dang 'dzin gyis bskyed pas na//mtshan ma'i gzhi med mtshan nyid med//'khor ba'i rgyun gyis khyer pa'i rnams//yang dang yang zhing skye ba yin//
299b1(mdo sde): chags med mtsho'i gter gyur la//gcig la gcig 'ju'i spre'u ltar yang //byed pa grub la gnas kyis mthong //'ju ba'i gnas dang rten med par//de bzhin rgyal ba rgyal 
299b2(mdo sde): sras rnams //sred pas rnam par dkrugs pa yi//rnam rtog sdug bsngal gnas pa'i rnams//sa'i gnas na nyon mongs phyir//rab tu byung dang phyir ldog dang //sbyin dang 
299b3(mdo sde): mchod pa dag kyang 'byung //'di 'dra rnal 'byor can gyi chos//rnam pa sna tshogs bsgom par gyis////'phags pa rgyan stug po/bam po gnyis pa//kun 
299b4(mdo sde): tu bzang po mi yi bdag/gzi brjid chen po sna tshogs gzugs//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che//rdo rje snying po la smras pa//'thun ba dag la dbang byed dang //
299b5(mdo sde): lha gnas 'khod pa gzhan dag dang //rin chen na khang pa sna tshogs na//gang yang stug po'i dkyil 'khod pa'i//rgyal sras rnal 'byor ldan ba dag /
299b6(mdo sde): gang gis stug ces bya thos te//thos nas nan tan byed pa'i rnams//gzhan du 'gyur la gzhol ba dag/gzhan du 'gyur ba'i tshul thos shing //ting nge 
299b7(mdo sde): 'dzin de'i tshul song ba//stug por ring po mi thogs 'gro/sangs rgyas yang dag 'gro la mos//'gro ba 'dzin dang shugs 'dzin dang //'jam dpal rnam par 
299b8(mdo sde): 'phrul ba dang //mthu chen 'phags pa 'jig rten dbang //zla ba la sogs gzugs dbang rgyal//blo gros shin tu zhi ba dang //lte ba rdo rje rnal 'byor rig /dru ma 
300a1(mdo sde): @#//mkha'i bdag po dang//phyag na rdo rje blo chen dang//rnal 'byor can dang rgyal sras rnams//gang yang bye ba'i zhing dag nas//chos mnyan phyir ni 'ong+sa 
300a2(mdo sde): pa'i rnams//stug po'i rgyan la gzhol ba dag/rgyal ba ma 'ongs mthong nas kyang//khong nas snying rje snying brtse ste//rnal 'byor ldan can dbang po'i gtso//sna tshogs 
300a3(mdo sde): gzugs la rab tu smras//rnal 'byor can gyi gtso la dris//'jigs med rdo rje snying po la//rin chen khang pa sna tshogs dang//dbang sgyur khang ba bzang por yang//ji ltar 
300a4(mdo sde): gang gis sna tshogs byas//sna tshogs gzugas can khyod kyis smros //dngos po kun gyi sna tshogs nyid//'gro ba dag gi rab 'byung ba//rdza la sogs pa'i 
300a5(mdo sde): dngos po dag/ji ltar chu 'jim thal zhar las//las su bya ba thams cad bskyed//'gro ba 'di dag de bzhin byas//rnga dang pi wang sgra la sogs//las
300a6(mdo sde): su bskyod las dbyangs 'byin te//de bzhin 'jig rten 'di la 'ang ci//bskyod pa'i las las kun 'byung ngam//rang bzhin gsum po ji bzhin du//dngos po gcig la 
300a7(mdo sde): kun tu gnas//sa rnams 'di ltar thams cadu //dngos dang dngos la rab tu gnas//de bzhin 'di dag kun kyang ci//dngos po gcig la rab gnas sam//gang phyir 'di ltar 
300a8(mdo sde): rgyan dag snang//khams gsum g.yo dang mi g.yor bcas//dga' ldan mtshe ma rab gnas dang//dbang sgyur rnams kyis bya(sa)sam ci//mi 'am ci dag du ma dang//gya nom 
300b1(mdo sde): snang bas byas sam ci //'og min thor tsugs can gyis sam//'on tam gzugs med rnams kyis kyang//lha rnams bdag po thams cad kyis//gcig du 'dus te 'di
300b2(mdo sde): byas sam//'phrul pa dag gis sam//rgyal ba khyu mchog gis mdzad dam//'on tam rgyal ba rgyal sras rnams//zhing gzhan rnam par bzhugs pas sam//rnal 'byor la 
300b3(mdo sde): mkhas rnal 'byor can //sangs rgyas zhing du bzhugs rnams kyis//sa rnams thams cad byas sam ci//gang la nor pa rab tu gnas//gang 'di nor pa snang ba dag/smig 
300b4(mdo sde): rgyu rnams la chu bzhin te//rdza ma'i phyogs la yon tan bzhin//'gro ba gang gis phyogs la gnas//yon tan mi 'bral y+ton can//yon tan med dang y+ton gnas//
300b5(mdo sde): me yin phan tshun 'brel pas na//y+ton tshogsu rnam par gnas//nor pa sna tshogs debzhin du//gang la 'ang de bzhin gnas yin nam//'gro ba rnam pa sna tshog+sa
300b6(mdo sde): rnams//sa rnams la ni gang snang ba//'on tam tshangs pas phyung ba 'am//'gro ba mthu bo ce 'is sam//khyu mchog gis sam spun zas sam//ser skya yis sam rang gis 
300b7(mdo sde): sam;;gces pa'i rkyen gyis byas sam pa//rang bzhin gyis sam dbang pos sam//dus dang phyogs kyis bskyed pa 'am//'on tam ma rig sred pas sam//lha yi drang srong 
300b8(mdo sde): the tsom za//lha'i tshogs kyang de bzhin te//grub dang dri za'i rgyal po rnams//sa la gnas pa'i rnal 'byor can//'on tam 'di dag mi 'phrul lam//'gro ba dri za 'dra zhig gam//rmi lam 
301a1(mdo sde): @#//yin nam smig rgyu 'am//sngon med rnam par rtog yin nam//gzung dang 'dzin pa'i dngos por yang //kha gnyis sprul bzhin gnas pa 'am//'on tam rang bzhin 
301a2(mdo sde): 'di med dam//'khrul 'khor ro langs bzhin gnas sam//mgal ba bskor ba 'dra ba 'am//'on tam skra yi tshogs lta bu 'am//byed dang las kyis ma byas shing //dbang po ku be ras 
301a3(mdo sde): ma byas//mchod sbyin sbyin bsreg chod 'bras sam//smrang gi gzhung gi lam gyis sam//rgyu rnams shin tu phal che bas//nan tan rtag pa ma yin te//'gro ba'i 'dzin pa med pa 'ang 
301a4(mdo sde): min//rgyu rnams kyi 'ang rgyu dge ste//'gro ba ngo bo nyid kyis grub//las kyi bag chags rnams las byung //rnam shes brgyad po'i skyes bu ni//kun gzhi zhes kyang bya ba ste//
301a5(mdo sde): des ni sne tshogs 'di dag kun//rdza ma'i 'khrul 'khor bzhin du bskor//til bkrum til mar ji bzhin du//lan tsa'i tsha bro ji lta bar//gzugs la mi rtag nyid ji 
301a6(mdo sde): bzhin//skyes bu 'di yang de bzhin gnas//ji ltar bsrung la sogs la dri//yon tan rnams la yon tan mtshan//gnyi zla gnyis kyi 'od zer bzhin//de ltar skyes bu gnas pa 
301a7(mdo sde): yin//dgos dang byed pa med pa ste//yod dang med kyi phyogs kyis spangs//gcig dang tha dad med pa ste//mur 'dug phyogs kyis rnam par spangs//rjes su 'gro ba'i shes pa med//
301a8(mdo sde): rnam yang rnam par mi rtag ste//grol ba'i so so rang rig pa//sangs rgyas sar skyes thub can gyis//gzung dang 'dzin pa med pa ste//snga med sems dang sems byung gis//rnam shes 
301b1(mdo sde): brgyad dang bral ba ste//nam yang rnam par mi brtag ste//de la gnas pa chu rlabs bzhin//phan tshun mtshan nyid tha mi dad//'byung zhing phyir yang ldog pa ste//de'i ldan ba rig pa min/
301b2(mdo sde): ji ltar thub pa'i rnal 'byor dang //rdzu 'phrul can gyi rdzu 'phrul bzhin//mnyam par bzhag pas rab tu mthong //rnal 'byor 'gro ba gzhan gyis min//de bzhin 'jug pa'i rnam shes
301b3(mdo sde): rnams//de dag kun gzhi lhan cig gnas//rnal 'byor can gyis rtag tu mthong //sangs rgyas sangs rgyas sras de bzhin//des ni 'di dag thams cad kyang //ji ltar nor bu phug pa'i

301b6(mdo sde): rgyu ba'i 'gro ba rnams//gang la 'jig rten 'di dag kun//nam mkha'i skar tshogs ji bzhin du//gnas shing rnam pa sna tshogs rnams//rlung gi rta yi shin tu bskor//ji ltar

301b8(mdo sde): gzhan yod min//rnam dag bar snang 'dra ba dag/dba' rlabs bzhin du rab tu 'byung //bag chags rnam pa'i nyes pa yis//mi chags pad+mo zla ba bzhin//pad+mo la ni chu med min//
302a1(mdo sde): @#//chu la zla gzugs med pa yang //chu song zla ba yod pa ste//pad+mo'i 'dab ma de bzhin du//mig la bud med gzhonu dag/mig gis mthong bar jir mi 'gyur//

302a3(mdo sde): yog par shin tu gnas//tshe dang rnam shes kun nas yog /drod kyang de bzhin stobs ces bgyis //sna tshogs gzugs kyi lus can gyi //rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs kyang rtsom

302b1(mdo sde): chags kyis//mi yi sems ni rab tu 'byung //grub pa'i don ni mi skye'o//thog ma med pa'i dus nas bsags//gnas ngan len gyi yul las byung //rab tu spros pas bsgos

302b8(mdo sde): rang gi lus la gnyed pa dang //phyogs kyi glang po bdag lus la//bdag gis sna yis chu 'thor dang //ji ltar shed can la la ngag/bdag gi lus la sos 'debs pa//
303a1(mdo sde): @#//de bzhin blo ni brtag don la//don gnyis tshul du 'jug pa'o//blo yi yul du bya ba 'di//khams gsum rnam par khyad par gnas//phyi dang nang gi bya ba yang//

303a5(mdo sde): 'dod dang gzugs dang gzugs med dang//'du shes med pa'i gnas rnams kyi//mun pa'i tshogs nas phyir byung ste//stug po de nas 'dir 'ongs so//sangs rgyas

303a8(mdo sde): po dri med bsod nams ste//sdig pa thams cad 'jig par byed//rnal 'byor can gyi gnas kyi mchog/sangs rgyas zhing gi dam pa phul//yul bus de yi sgra thos te//
303b1(mdo sde): thos nas bdag cag dga' ba'i yid//stug po rnal 'byor can gnas kyi//brtan lam bdag cag thob par bya//'og min tshangs pa thor tsugs can//gtsang ma'i gnas kyis

310a3(mdo sde): 'bras bu dang//gang gzhan mchod sbyin 'bras bu dag/phyugs bcings pa yi 'bras bu yang//tshangs pa'i 'jig rten nas ldog go/chos gsum can gyi 'gro ba
310a4(mdo sde): dag/ci nyes mi rig thor tsugs can//yon tan gsum gyi 'bru 'brel pa//chu skyes bzhin du snying po med//'di yis thar pa mi rnyed pa//stug po de bzhin gshegs
310a5(mdo sde): pa'i gnas//de ni rnal 'byor gyis thob ste//stug po rnal 'byora rig pa'i gnas//de bas tshud cig tshangspa'i bdag/rnal 'byor can rnams 'jug 
310a6(mdo sde): brtson ba//'di la bdag gnyen gang dag kyang//skye zhing 'chi ba yong med de//mi chod gzhig du med pa ni//lus can kun gyi rnam par shes//bag chags kyis ni mi gos
310a7(mdo sde): pa||sngon gyi dri mas nyi zla la//de bzhin yid kyi gnas ngan len bsags pa//spros pas kyang ngo cing mi 'dug/ji ltar lha rdzas chu skyes dang//namakha' la yang phyag ngar dang//sprin
