310b1(mdo sde): rdzas rta rnal 'byor can gyi tshul skyed pa//tshul khrims can gyi tshul 'thungs te//ye shes can gyis shes 'thungs shing //mchog gis ye shes 'thungs nas kyang //
310b2(mdo sde): 'khor ba las ni 'da' bar gyis//'di la khams rnams gang yang med//phung po lnga dang rnam shes kyang //ming dang gzugs dag rgyur byas shing //lus can rnams kyi
310b3(mdo sde): yid kyang skye//ji ltar me ni shing dbang gis//'bar ba 'bar bar rtog pa med//de bzhin phung po shing rta stong //ci phyir rnal 'byor 'jug mi bya//ri dwags sred dang dri za yi//
310b4(mdo sde): gnas dang sgra tshogs snang ba ru//ri dwags bus pa la sogs mthong //yang dag ma yin bltas bslad pas//de bzhin yul las byung pa'i sems //khab long
310b5(mdo sde): lta bur 'khor ba ste//gang yang 'di la dngos med pa//'byung ba rnams dang 'byung pa yi//g.yo ba dro ba gsher sra bar//bus pa rnams ni rtag tu rtog/'byung
310b6(mdo sde): ba sems kyi tshogs las na//ci phyir rnal 'byor 'ju mi bya//ci tsam ma zhig lus yod dang //ji tsam du ni blo 'byung pa//de bar rnal 'byor ye shes la//ma bshol bsam
310b7(mdo sde): gtan rab tu gyis//thams cad sgyu ma rmi lam ltar//'byung ba gsum kun rab tu ltos//smig rgyu chu zla lta bu ni//ci phyir rnal 'byor 'jog mi bya//ji bzhin dri za'i grong

311a1(mdo sde): @#//de bzhin phung po'i gnas yinan//ci phyir rnal 'byor 'jug mi bya//sa 'di sa las sogs pa'i tshogs//'dus pa rdul gyi phung po yin//'di sems
311a2(mdo sde): sems byung tshogs yin bar//rgyal ba'i sras rnams kun ltos shig/'byung ba chen po bzhi po rnams//phan tshun kun nas 'das pa ste//gdeg pa la sogs ldan ba yin//
311a3(mdo sde): ci phyir rnal 'byor 'jug ma bya//'di ni rlung dang 'khris pas myos//ro bslab lta bur gnas pa ste//byed pa gang yang yod ma yin//ci phyir rnal 'byor mjug mi bya//dbu ba'i 
311a4(mdo sde): gong bu lta bu kun//'gro ba gnas shing g.yo ba rnams//smig rgyu 'dra ba'i 'du shesu//rdza la sogs pa shing rta rtogs//tshor ba chu bur lta bu ste//bde dang mi bder rtog pa yin//chu

311a7(mdo sde): khams gsum 'gro dang mi 'gro ba//'di kun blo yis rtogs bya ba//'jigs med rgyal ba'i sras rnams kyis//nyes pa'i rgyu ni rtsa dang bcas//ye shes me yis rab 
311a8(mdo sde): bsregs na//lha rdzas bde ba yod par 'gro/rnal 'byor can gnas stug por yang //'jigs med 'di ni mtshan ma med//mtshan ma bcings pa'i ngo bo can//
311b1(mdo sde): mtshan ma med pa rtag tu bde//mtshan ma sems kyi spyod yul lo//sems kyi spyod yul bden ba min//bden ba blo gros spyod yul te//sems ni shes rab spyod yul te//

311b4(mdo sde): pa'i bde ba dang//mthong pa'i chos la gnas pa dang//bdag med pa ru shes nyid kyang//'dod chags bcom bar gyur pa na'o//de nas mi bdag rin chen gtsug/
311b5(mdo sde): blo chen byang chub sems dpa' yis//rnal 'byor can kun dbang la smras//rdo+e'i snying po 'jigs med la//bye ba'i zhing rnams thaMd na//rgyal sras khyod ni 
311b6(mdo sde): brtson ba ste//rgyal ba rnams la khyod tshade //rnal 'byor can la nye bar gnas//rab 'byor the tsom gcad pa ste//dpe rnams grub par bya ba'i phyir//brtag bya yan lag thaMd 
311b7(mdo sde): dag/lus can rnams kyi lus skyes pa//tshul ni sgra yis brjod par mdzad//tshig du brjod par jir mi bgyi//gnad dang zung gis rnam par mdzod//mi zin pa yi rgyu 
311b8(mdo sde): rnams dang//bgab zungs nor gyis gzhol bar gyis//phung po'i rgyu rnams bsal bar mdzod//yang dag 'gyur ba med pa dang//dge ba yis kyang mkhas paso //ring po mi 
312a1(mdo sde): @#//thogs thar pa'i thob//phung po'i rgyu rnams bsregs nasu //chos min chos kyis tshe 'byung ste//'di dang gzhan du'ang 'thob par 'gyur//shes p+sa

312a3(mdo sde): dang ||rnam shes yul la mngon gyur pas//myur phyir khong du mi chud pa//spyod yul du ni sems rgyu ba'o//yid sngon 'gro ba chos rnams te//lus can kun gyi de bzhino/
312a4(mdo sde): yid 'gro yid ni gces pa dag/yid du 'ong bas cir mi bshad//gang dang gang gis rdzas kyis su //mi rnams stug por 'grub pa yi//des ni de yi dngos 'gyur ba/
312a5(mdo sde): sna tshogs gzugs kyi nor bu bzhin //ji ltar nora bu sna tshogs gzugs //mtshon dang 'byor na rnam par mdzes//rnal 'byor can dbus khyod de bzhin //

312b1(mdo sde): pa brtan pa mi 'jig pa//skye can kun gyi rnam par shes rgyu yis sun 'byung ma yin ba//'dra dang mi 'dra rnams kyis te//dam bcas yan lag rnams kyi yang//mtha' 
312b2(mdo sde): ma thub pa med par 'gyur //dam bcas pa yi dam bca' dang//rgyu yi khyad par rgyu dag ste//de bas de la rgal du med//rtog pa can gyi spyod yul min//rnam par shes
312b3(mdo sde): pa gzhan gyur na//de ni stug po sems spangs pa'o//rtog can rnams gyi yul ma yin//de bzhin gshegs pa'i pho brang stug/ma skyes sngon dang mtha' ma med //

313a1(mdo sde): @#//mi shes las kyang smros pa min//khur khyer khur gyi yul yin te//'di ni sangs rgyas rnams kyi yul//lha rdzas de bzhin gshegs kyi mkhyen//
